## Decisions under the 2026-10-02 policy (orchestrator; each MADE here, measured by the battery score)

**DT-1 — The score instrument (MADE 2026-10-02 17:50).** `verify/score.py` reads a battery directory (12 legs × reps) and emits the tuple
`(legs_identical, outside_mask_divergent_words, pins_green)`: `legs_identical` = legs whose frames/audio/mem lanes are IDENTICAL end to end
(under the declared masks) on every rep; `outside_mask_divergent_words` = the sum over the PROBE PAIRS of RAM0+SPAD words differing outside
the mask at the same boundary index (probe pairs = OLD images at boundary 2 and 60 of every anchor and 60/120 of coldboot, produced once and
frozen under `verify/baseline/<leg>/probe/`, against NEW images from `--state-every` probe legs run with the battery binary); `pins_green` =
the full ctest suite's pass count. A change is KEPT only if the tuple strictly improves lexicographically with no pin/test regression.
Rationale: the user's score ordering made measurable; the probe pairs use the proved pairing (OLD `state_b<N>` = boundary N+1, NEW label =
boundary). **Measured outcome:** the tool (stage I8.13-T).

**DT-2 — Batteries run on the Release binary with an identity guard (MADE 2026-10-02 17:50).** Evidence: I8.12-B/I8.11-B — the Release
build's deterministic lanes equal the Debug build's (coldboot 200 frames: frames/mem/audio/drawir/gsops identical) and NEW is STABLE 12/12
over 3 reps; a Debug 12-leg rep costs ≈ 75 min, a Release rep ≈ 10 min. Choice: `battery.py` runs on `build-release/app/host/nativeee`; every
battery is preceded by the guard `RootReleaseLanesIdentical` (Release vs Debug: coldboot 200 frames + artdet 60 frames, three lanes + drawir
`cmp`); a guard failure voids the battery. The Debug build remains the ctest/pin binary. Rationale: the score needs 3 reps per decision;
identity is proved per run, not assumed. **Measured outcome:** per battery (`guard=IDENTICAL` line in each battery's summary).

**DP-8 (2)(b) — DECISION MADE (2026-10-02 17:50):** §7.1 row `P4-CKPT120-OLD-SESSION14-POPULATE-ALLOCS`, no mask. OLD-side cause named
(I8.10-B): OLD's `0x268440 [SESSION-14]` hook APPENDS a second type-registry populate (`FUN_0025c6f8`) and its `0x1b36b8 [RENDINIT]` hook frees
the first run's DMA-tag arrays — six hook-induced allocations at F:96 that retail never makes; NEW runs the title's one natural populate
(`FUN_0025c730 ← FUN_00268440`, allocations #58–62 equal on both sides). Rationale: the title's own code populates once. The heap-tail
words after F:96 are this row's; the mem lane window after 120 is re-baselined from NEW under this row ONLY after the residual words at the
120 probe are 100 % attributed (DP-20 class rule). **Measured outcome:** gate run 5.

**DP-8 (5) — DECISION MADE:** row "STREAM client call/reply counters and slot bytes are lap-count-derived" (attribute, no mask) — a consequence
of the poll-count class (DP-19): the counters count the title's polls. Decided with DP-19.

**DP-15 — DECISION MADE (2026-10-02 17:50): (a).** Row `P4-CKPT120-OLD-PTC-STUB-SLOTS` + mask `0x01f00000-0x01fc0000` (HI exclusive). The
permitted mask class: a NAMED OLD host-only writer (`recompiler.cpp:8509` `STUB_BASE 0x01F00000`, stride `0x40000`: OLD writes synthesized `.ptc`
view streams into a reserved top-of-RAM region the retail title never touches) into memory retail never touches; the hashmap consequence
words stay unmasked and attributed. **Measured outcome (2026-10-02 20:40): KEPT.** Row `P4-CKPT120-OLD-PTC-STUB-SLOTS` (EQUIVALENCE_PLAN §7.1) + mask range `0x01f00000-0x01fc0000` in `verify/mask/coldboot_elf_text.ranges`. Score on the S0 probes (coldboot pairs, Release copy, same images, mask before → after): b60 12547/89 unchanged, b120 RAM0 443219 → 424679 (−18,540 words = the three slot images, 6,180 words each); legs_identical 0 → 0, pins unaffected (a mask edit). `int_work/gates/dp15/`.

**DP-17 — DECISION MADE (2026-10-02 17:50): (a)**, the user's order. Host-served loader completions become visible AT THE POLL THAT ASKS once
their modelled due time (the CD latency model, emulated time) has passed — not at the next EE boundary (today's `DeliveryMeter`: one read
per boundary; the Beach hub load that exits boot case 6 takes ≈ 4,900 boundaries instead of 852). Hardware rationale: the DVD completes
asynchronously while the EE spins; the EE's poll observes a completed read the moment it polls. Toggle: Config `cdvd.delivery_visibility =
boundary | poll` (default `poll` once KEPT). Rejected: (b) boundary-only + 6,000-frame legs (OLD-shaped), (c) a k-per-boundary budget (no
hardware rationale). Expected: coldboot reaches case 7 (title screen, W142 music) near OLD's boundary 852; DP-19's poll count changes too
(each lap's poll now consumes completed reads). **Measured outcome (2026-10-02 22:00): OPEN after three attempts (the rule).** Attempt 1 (I8.13-A): poll SLOWER than boundary (the wait seam force-completed 904/906 reads; `latency_scale=0` pinned) and a stall at frame 266 (BUG-068). Attempt 2 (I8.14-A): forced completions removed, census honest, `cdvd.poll_latency_scale`; still slower (the title's 901-read synchronous burst inside one frame; the model dated it 1.9 s of drive time without advancing frames) and the stall. Attempt 3 (I8.15-A): blocking waits park whole frames (`cdvd_wait_parks=117/117`, overruns 0), BUG-068 FIXED (the HLE wait now runs the title's completion copy under poll) — the stall is gone, but poll is still not faster (907 vs 918 reads at 300 frames: `LoaderSpinLift` yields after ONE poll per lap, so the title's loop still consumes one read per boundary; the lift would have to iterate while the drive completes reads inside the frame) and a new `FATAL-NOJUMP pc=0` at frame 309 under poll (same shape as f57292's park — a NEW defect the faster boot exposes; → DP-22). The components stay behind `cdvd.delivery_visibility` (default `boundary`); no battery was run with `poll` (the 309 crash makes coldboot RUN-FAIL = WORSE). Class OPEN for the score; the playability consequence (an ≈ 80 s boot load in play mode) is carried to Phase 8 with the attempt-4 shape named.

**DP-17 (a) — IMPLEMENTED behind the toggle (I8.13-A, 2026-10-02; default OFF = today's behaviour until the gate KEEPs it).** Toggle: Config
`cdvd.delivery_visibility = boundary | poll` (default `boundary`; `--set cdvd.delivery_visibility=poll`). Model (`engine/cdvd/delivery_meter.{h,cpp}`,
`engine/cdvd/hle/loader_model.{h,cpp}`): the virtual drive's timeline in ms — a read issued during tick k starts at `max(drive free, k × 16.667)`,
ends at start + seek + transfer (the Modelled cost; `latency_scale` when > 0, unscaled at 0); `DeliveryMeter::CompleteNow(k)` (run by every
`LoaderModel::Poll` through the root-bound `SetNowTick` = `EmuDomain::DomainTick`) delivers the reads whose end lies before `(k + 1) × 16.667`
AT THE POLL (trace `dlv src=poll`; the `DeliveryBudget` is not consulted under `poll` — the drive model is the throughput, ≈ 6–9 of the title's
2–3-sector windows per frame); a read ending later keeps its slot-4 due at the tick the drive finishes in. `boundary` = today bit for bit
(`RootPlayPolicyInert` PASS on the stage's binary; `CompleteNow` returns 0 and touches nothing). Census `cdvd_visibility=`, `cdvd_poll_deliveries=`,
`cdvd_boundary_deliveries=`. Pins: `DeliveryMeter` (a 2 KB contiguous read issued at tick 10 ends at 167.6 ms and is `CompleteNow(10)`; a long-seek
read is due at 17 in both modes and lands at the first poll of tick 16; `boundary` never completes within the issue tick) + `LoaderModelAndDeliveryBudget`
(`TestPollVisibility`: Poll delivers/consumes with a 1-byte budget, the held read still lifts the spin, the unpolled read is delivered by slot 4;
`boundary` with a NowTick bound is HeldInflight as today). **Measured (I8.13-A, before the gate):** the coldboot `--frames 300` leg under `poll`
STALLS at frame 266 (≈ 26 boundaries into the Beach load): no boundary for > 6 min, the EE thread in the title's allocator `FUN_002cbf98` walking a
free list whose link reads 0 (`lldb`: `memory::read<unsigned int>(address=4)` at `recompiled_shard_031.cpp:4806`, stack `FUN_0017d6b8 → FUN_002aa8d0
→ FUN_002b4c10 → FUN_002b4738 → FUN_002abec8 → … → FUN_002c7718 → FUN_002cbf98`) — **BUG-068 (OPEN)**: a NEW defect exposed by in-frame
delivery (OLD ran 23 completions per frame through its pump without it), not attributed in the stage; the root pin
`tests/app/root_cdvd_poll_visibility.py` is written but NOT registered (it would be red). The gate cannot KEEP `poll` before BUG-068 is attributed
and fixed (attempt 1 of 3 on this class). **Measured outcome:** gate run 5 after BUG-068.

**DP-17 (a) — attempt 2 of 3 (I8.14-A, 2026-10-02; `int_work/i814a/`; default still `boundary`, `boundary` bit-identical: `RootPlayPolicyInert … Passed`, bnd300 lanes = i813a/review `bnd250`/`det200` on every complete window).** *Decisions MADE:* (1) the poll drive's time is its own key `cdvd.poll_latency_scale` (> 0, default 1.0, NOT pinned by the deterministic profile — whose `cdvd.latency_scale=0` is `boundary`'s "no drive time"); the former "a zero scale reads as unscaled" rule is gone. (2) The title's blocking wait under `poll` delivers the read AT THE DRIVE'S END (the EE spins until the drive is done — what the console does): `LoaderModel::CompleteNow(fs)` applies the payload, SETTLES the meter entry (`DeliveryMeter::Settle`: no phantom pop later) and counts `WaitDelivered`; nothing is forced (`cdvd_forced=0` under `poll`); a wait whose drive end lies at or past the frame's edge is an OVERRUN (`cdvd_wait_overruns`: the console's EE would have missed the vblank; NEW's frames keep ticking) and the drive timeline keeps that time (rejected alternatives: clamping `busy_until` to the frame's edge = dropping drive work the drive did; not charging waited reads = the reviewer's second option, rejected for the same reason). (3) The census is the MODEL's split by landing site (`cdvd_poll_deliveries` / `cdvd_boundary_deliveries` / `cdvd_wait_deliveries`; the meter's raw pops stay as `cdvd_meter_pops`), + `cdvd_seeks=contig/short/long` and a per-leg `loader_trace.log` under `poll`. (4) A poll delivery is data + status only — verified by code: the `apply` closure writes dest, the ring + cursor and the slot byte; no RPC end-callback / ISR runs inside a `Poll` (they are slot-4 and wait-pump duties), so nothing had to be deferred. *Measured (deterministic coldboot, same binary):* `boundary` 300 frames `loader_reads=5/918/3138916`, `cdvd_boundary_deliveries=15 cdvd_forced=903 cdvd_meter_pops=0/918`, boot case `b100:2 b150:4 b200:6 b250:6`, `loader_spin_lifts=lap1=11,lap2=15,declines=233`, `mem.mhash 60,0x2c5b6d50e6b67abf 120,0x18ce63c76bf63e84`; `poll` 250 frames `loader_reads=5/906/2748836`, `cdvd_poll_deliveries=2 cdvd_wait_deliveries=904 cdvd_wait_overruns=904 cdvd_forced=0 cdvd_seeks=contig=897,short=7,long=2`, case `b100:1 b150:2 b200:4 b250:6`, `lap2=137`, `mem.mhash 60,0x910e12a5c580878c 120,0xc01e01cee6cb85c0`; `poll` 300 frames STALLS at frame 266 (BUG-068, reproduced on the new model). **`poll` is still SLOWER than `boundary`, and the trace names why — not seeks, not the wait seam's accounting:** 901 of the 906 reads are issued AND waited at ONE domain tick (121: `poll250c/loader_trace.log` has 3606 lines at t121; `declines=184` ≪ 901, so the burst is inside one loader-SM poll, not the lap loop) — a synchronous bulk load the console would spend ≈ 1.9 s of blocked EE on (≈ 55 dropped frames), which NEW runs inside one 16.7 ms frame; the drive timeline honestly ends at 3910 ms, so the other stream's read is held 110 frames (`yld` t122…t230) and waited at t231. Then the domain tick jumps 231 → 1322 (`ticks=demand=1071`: demand ticks fired inside the load — the OLD-defect class the policy names) and the next read, enqueued at tick 1322, lands 15 frames later at the poll of frame 266 — the stall follows that delivery immediately (BUG-068 narrowed; `docs/bugs/BUG-068-…`). [Corrected in attempt 3: the 15-frame latency is that read's own modelled drive time — 262.9 ms = `end=22296.699` − 1322 × 16.667, a long seek plus 163 sectors — measured from its issue tick in the same domain the poll checks; the demand-tick jump before the issue does not change it. The jump is an independent OLD-defect-class item, not a cause here.] *Why not KEPT:* the score cannot improve while `poll` loses to `boundary` on the case word and stalls; the two residual causes are outside this stage's model: (α) a frame cannot stretch while the EE is blocked (the burst) — the honest options are a boundary raised from inside the wait (the console's dropped frames: vblank ISR + IOP + audio keep running) or an engine-level EE-instant that the headroom query sees, both timing-domain design (`engine/core/time`, the sequencer); (β) the demand ticks moving the domain tick inside a frame (not owned here; attempt 3: an independent item, not a cause of the dated delivery — see the correction above). *Expected throughput from the model:* contiguous 2-sector windows 1.85 ms → ≈ 8 per frame if the lap were time-bounded; the title's burst is not. Pins added: `LoaderModelAndDeliveryBudget` `TestPollWaitSettles` (7 waited contiguous reads at tick 10 → 7 `WaitDelivered`, 0 forced, 0 overruns, 0 phantoms; the 8th polled lands within tick 10 — the reviewer's pin; a waited long seek = 1 overrun, settled, the next issue starts on the busy drive; boundary mode's wait still `Forced`). `RootCdvdPollVisibility` stays UNREGISTERED (its "≥ 4× reads" and "no stall to 300" assertions are red by measurement); its census keys are updated. **Measured outcome:** OPEN after attempt 2 — not KEPT, not REVERTED (default unchanged); attempt 3 needs (α)/(β) decided first.

**DP-17 (a) — attempt 3 of 3 (I8.15-A, 2026-10-02; `int_work/i815a/`; default still `boundary`, `boundary` bit-identical: `bnd300f` vs `i814a/bnd300` and `i814a/review/bnd300` `frames.fhash/mem.mhash/drawir.ohash/gsops.ohash IDENTICAL over 300`, `audio.ahash IDENTICAL over 50`; `RootPlayPolicyInert` in the full suite).** *Decision MADE — (α) resolved inside the model:* **a blocking disc wait spans frames.** Under `poll`, when the title blocks on a read whose modelled drive end lies at or past the current frame's edge, `LoaderModel::CompleteNow(fs)` PARKS the EE for whole frames through the owner's `ParkFrame` (`app/host/ee_bootstrap.cpp`: `BoundaryProtocol::Wait` repeated until the domain tick moved by one — the FMV pace park's and the kernel timer wait's mechanism: T0 + 1 field, VBON/TIM2/VBOF raised, no sub-tick; under the deterministic policy a demanded field the quantum absorbs advances nothing and is repeated, a demand from a non-guest thread ends the park) and re-checks, until the end is inside the frame; then the read is delivered at the drive's end and settled. Hardware rationale: the console's EE is blocked in the sync wait while vblanks pass. Rejected: a boundary raised from inside the wait (would count frames and run the Verify slot inside a guest wait — the sequencer's, not owned), and keeping the overrun (attempt 2: the frame edge crossed inside the wait). Census `cdvd_wait_parks=<frames>/<waits>`; trace `prk … end= edge=` per parked frame; `enq … lba= sec= end=` (the drive timeline auditable per read — the reviewer's MAJOR 2). *The (β) correction* is above. *BUG-068 ATTRIBUTED and FIXED* (the reviewer's MAJOR 1; the diag `--watch-word` leg run through `int_work/i815a/lldb_watch_flush.py` because the census only flushes at the frames cap): the root cause is NEW's HLE wait omitting the retail wait's completion step (the callback stream's copy, then `fs+0x10 = 0`), so the staging of the last ring window outlived it and the title's poll-path completion `FUN_002ad110` re-ran a stale 0xa16e-byte copy into re-used heap when a later DIRECT (≥ ring cap) window landed at a poll — `w268b/watch.log` b265: `FUN_002b5dd8+0x508` cursor `0x972340->0x97c4ae`, `FUN_002ad110+0x14d0` `fs+0x10 0x1185940->0x0`, then `FUN_002c6ce0+0x3e4` zeroing `0x1185940`; fix: the wait runs the title's completion copy and clears the staging (poll visibility only; `loader_wait_completion_copies=902`). *Measured (same binary, deterministic coldboot, `--frames 300`):* `boundary` `loader_reads=5/918/3138916 cdvd_forced=903 cdvd_wait_overruns=0 cdvd_wait_parks=0/0`, case `b100:2 b150:4 b200:6 b250:6`, `loader_spin_lifts=lap1=11,lap2=15,declines=233`, `mem.mhash 60,0x2c5b6d50e6b67abf 120,0x18ce63c76bf63e84`; `poll` `loader_reads=5/907/3079436 cdvd_poll_deliveries=4 cdvd_wait_deliveries=903 cdvd_wait_overruns=0 cdvd_wait_parks=117/117 cdvd_forced=0 cdvd_seeks=contig=897,short=7,long=3`, case `b100:1 b150:4 b200:6 b250:6` (attempt 2: `b150:2 b200:4`), `lap2=37,declines=235`, `ticks=boundary=300,demand=1188` (1071 FMV + 117 parks), `mem.mhash 60,0x87f09fce0913dda5 120,0x762d7b4eb61ad7e9`, `frames=300 finalized=1` — the frame-266 stall is gone and the burst spans ticks 121…229 (109 parked frames) as the drive time says. The `poll --frames 1200` leg ends at frame 309 with `[FATAL-NOJUMP] could-not-jump pc=0x0` (before AND after the BUG-068 fix; the trace's last event is the title polling a 1-sector long-seek read `enq fs=0xbb3560 lba=522998 sec=1 end=24963.422` at tick 1491, `yld` to 1496) — a NEW gap the load reaches only under `poll` (boundary mode's leg has never been this far into the Beach load by frame 1500), rowed OPEN (INT-I815A-POLL-F309-NOJUMP) with the diag watch leg as the next evidence; the case-7 edge and the W142 onset are therefore not measured. Pins: `LoaderModelAndDeliveryBudget` `TestPollWaitParks` (a long seek waited at tick 10 parks 6 frames, no overrun, entry settled; a 40-read burst parks exactly the edges the drive crossed; a park that cannot advance time → one attempt then overrun; unbound → overrun; boundary mode never parks); `RootCdvdPollVisibility` REGISTERED (TIMEOUT 600) with the measured assertions (both legs finalize; `cdvd_forced=0 cdvd_wait_overruns=0 cdvd_wait_parks>0`; every read delivered once; reads ≥ 0.9× boundary's — the "4×" premise never held, boundary mode force-completes 903 waits; case ≥ 6 and not below boundary's). **Measured outcome:** attempt 3 landed behind the toggle; `poll` now matches `boundary` on the case word to b250 and no longer stalls to 300, but is not yet faster (907 vs 918 reads; the burst is the title's) and the 1200-frame leg crashes at 309 — the orchestrator scores; not KEPT by this stage, OPEN with one named follow-up.

**DP-17 (a) — attempt 4 of 4 (P8.1-A, 2026-10-03; `int_work/p81a/`; default still `boundary`, `boundary` bit-identical by construction: the only poll-path change is inside `if (visibility == Poll)` branches; `RootPlayPolicyInert` + the full suite).** *Hardware constants, checked against the reference (PCSX2 `pcsx2/CDVD/CDVD.cpp`, fetched 2026-10-03):* `tbl_ContigiousSeekDelta[DVD] = 16` = `kContiguousSectors`; `tbl_FastSeekDelta[DVD single] = 14764` = `kShortSeekSectors`; `Cdvd_FastSeek_Cycles = PSXCLK×30/1000` (30 ms) and `Cdvd_FullSeek_Cycles = PSXCLK×100/1000` (100 ms) = the 30/100 ms seek terms; the transfer `kSectorsPerMs = 1.08` is PCSX2's CAV model at the INNER track: `cdvdBlockReadTime`: `sectorSpeed = (lba/2298496)×0.60 + 0.40`, `cycles = PSXCLK / (DVD_SECTORS_PERSECOND(675) × Speed(4) × sectorSpeed)` → 675 × 4 × 0.40 = 1080 sectors/s. So OLD's `DeliveryMeter` constants are the reference's, not a calibration — except that the reference's transfer rate RISES with the LBA's radius (at lba 522998, the level file: 0.5365 → 1.449 sectors/ms; at the disc's outer edge 2.7/ms) and the `+12 ms` rotational term is a fixed stand-in for PCSX2's `cdvdRotationalLatency` (half a rotation at the LBA's rpm). The CAV radius term is NOT landed in this stage (every numeric pin of `DeliveryMeter`/`LoaderModel` encodes 1.08/ms; re-pinning them is the next step, named in the HANDOFF), so the poll drive stays the inner-track constant-rate model — pessimistic for the Beach load (which lies past lba 500000 → ≈ 1.45/ms on the reference). *The three observation points:* the title's poll and slot 4 were in order already; the blocking wait was not (DP-22's decision (1) above) — landed. *The lift (rule 2):* `LoaderSpinLift` already iterates while completions land inside the frame: a poll whose read the drive finished is `Progress` (the meter's `CompleteNow(tick)` runs first), `ShouldYieldPollSlice` declines (`!HeldInflight` / `!InFlight`) and the title's loop runs its next lap; it yields only for a read whose end lies past the frame's edge — pinned now (`TestLiftIteratesInsideTheFrame`: three contiguous reads inside one frame → 3 polls Progress, 0 yields; the 4th past the edge → 1 yield). The census of the 300-frame legs (`cdvd_poll_deliveries=4`, `cdvd_wait_deliveries=903`) is therefore the TITLE's doing: through frame 300 its loads are synchronous waits (the FMV/boot burst), not polls — case-6 throughput under poll is measurable only past frame 309, i.e. after BUG-069. *Measured this stage:* the DP-22 attribution leg only (`w1/`: `frames=309`, the same park). The 1500-frame poll leg, the case-7 boundary, the title-screen hash, the W142 onset and the probe counts are NOT measured — blocked by the frame-309 park (BUG-069, root cause narrowed to "notify before the job's read landed", the NEW path still unnamed). **Measured outcome: attempt 4 lands the in-order wait + two pins behind the toggle; the class stays OPEN (no battery can be run under `poll` while coldboot parks at 309).** The rule's three attempts are spent on the drive model; the remaining item is a NEW defect (BUG-069) in the loader SM's `isDone` path under poll, not the drive model — tracked under BUG-069 / DP-22, attempt 2 of 3.

**DP-17 (a) — attempt-4 measurements (P8.2-A, 2026-10-03; `int_work/p82a/poll1500/`; same dev binary as the DP-22 attempt-3 fix, `--frames 1500 --state-every 60`, deterministic, soft backend).** With BUG-069 fixed the poll leg passes frame 309 and runs to **frame 624 in ≈45 s, then stops advancing frames (BUG-070: the title's load-wait loop spins inside one frame with no host read in flight; two lldb samples inside `FUN_002aa8d0` from `FUN_0017d6b8`, inner frames `FUN_002a9898`/`FUN_002a9e88`; the last 23 reads were 1-sector reads of the same sector `lba=527124`, every one delivered and consumed)**. Measured to 624: boot case by image `b0:0 b60:0 b120:2 b180:5 b240:6 b300:6 … b600:6` (boundary mode at 300 frames: `b100:2 b150:4 b200:6`; the case-7 boundary is NOT reached); `mem.mhash` `60,0x8318c0db2eff7a82 120,0xc844f22eae166926` (poll; S2's boundary values `60,0x2c5b6d50e6b67abf 120,0x18ce63c76bf63e84` — poll mode's images differ by construction: the parked frames and the served slot byte); loader trace `2730 enq = 2730 dlv = 2730 cns`, `117 prk`, `679 yld`, 0 `rej`, 9–18 polls per tick at ticks 1805–1812 (the lift iterating inside the frame as pinned). NOT measurable past the 624 spin: the case-7 boundary, the title-screen hash, `snd_kon`, the W142 audio onset, `iop_cdvd reads`, `cdvd_completions_per_frame`, `loader_polls_per_frame` (the census flushes only at finalize). MEASURED (corrected after the P8.2-A review, MAJOR 1 — the probes lie at boundaries 2/60/120, before the spin; the reviewer ran `verify/probe_images.py --legs coldboot,artdet_f37152 --set cdvd.delivery_visibility=poll` on the landed dev binary and scored with `verify/score.py` against the frozen OLD probes, `int_work/p82a/review/{probes_poll,probes_bnd,score_poll.json,score_bnd.json}`): **poll** coldboot b60 RAM0 **125** / SPAD 89 (image `0x8318c0db2eff7a82`), b120 RAM0 **433344** / SPAD 171 (`0xc844f22eae166926`); artdet b2 64/98, b60 400/97; `outside_mask_words=434388`. **boundary** (same binary) coldboot b60 **12547**/89, b120 **424679**/120 (= S2, `0x2c5b6d50e6b67abf`/`0x18ce63c76bf63e84`), artdet 64/98, 400/97; `outside_mask_words=438094`. Poll vs boundary: b60 −12,422 RAM0 words, b120 +8,665 RAM0 / +51 SPAD, artdet images byte-IDENTICAL (`cmp new_b2/new_b60`). The +8,665 at b120 is attributed in P8.3-A (below). Also: the P8.2-A `poll1500` leg ran with `--set video.backend=soft`, under which every `frames.fhash` line carries one constant hash (`0x1d609d1b729f12a4`) — the title-screen frame hash must be measured without the soft backend or by the `drawir` census (review MINOR 5). **Measured outcome: DP-17 stays OPEN; the blocker moved from BUG-069 (fixed) to BUG-070 (observed, not attributed — the (α) class at a poll loop that waits on something other than a host-served read; next leg named in BUG-070).**

**DP-17 (a) — attempt-4 measurements, continued: BUG-070 attributed (P8.3-A, 2026-10-03; `int_work/p83a/`; no model change landed — diagnostics only, `boundary` bit-identical: the dev build's `RootPlayPolicyInert` + the `det200` cmp below).** *The spin's writers (diag binary, `--watch-flush-from 620` — the new harness knob that rewrites `watch.log` every 256 recorded stores from a boundary on, because the spin never reaches the end-of-leg flush; `spin2/watch.log`, 21 words: the three fs objects' word 0, the whole STREAM slot table `0x3b3d00..2f`, the countdowns, the Beach sub0 state, the STREAM open latch `0x309bf4`, the batch count `0x3b27ea`):* the spinning frame writes 12 lines — the last 1-sector window's delivery and the title's completion (`FUN_002ad110+0x161c` fs `0x30300->0x20300`), ONE `Ship(0)` (`FUN_001e2958+0xea4` latch `-1->9` = the cmd-9 status query, then `FUN_001e13b0+0x13fc` `9->-1`) — and nothing more while it spins (no flush in > 75 s): no fs flag, no slot byte, no record staged, no sub-state write. *The image behind the frame (`s624/state_b623.ps2st`, `ram_dump.py`):* case 6; Beach sub0 `0xc1aa20` state 3 since b302 (its reads are the 2,700 host-served windows), the loader SM `0xbb2560` holding a read job with 35 ring jobs queued, fs `0xbb3560` carrying a 22-byte window (`dest=0x171f400 size=0x16`, the last of 23 sub-sector windows of header sector `lba=527124`), slot 0 = `0x10`, every other slot 0. *What the lap waits on:* candidate (a). `SceSif::CallRpc` completes the title's status call synchronously but queues its END CALLBACK (`FUN_001e6968` → the reply processor `FUN_001e1f48`) for `RpcRouter::CompletePending`, which runs at boundary slot 4 or inside the wait seam's `pump_module` — never inside the title's poll loop; the IOP kernel (the module's CD worker, `CdvdmanModule`'s `sceCdRead` completions at `NowUs()+3000`, the hard timer) is pumped at slot 5 / the wait seam only. The lap (`FUN_002aa8d0`: nonzero while the entry counts differ and a loader SM reports progress) therefore iterates in memory on frozen T0 with the module's reply never processed and the module never advancing; `LoaderSpinLift` declines (no host read in flight; `declines=2679` by frame 624) and the frame cannot end. On the console the SIF interrupt lands the reply mid-frame and the IOP streams concurrently — the clock model's second clause ("IOP progress … made visible at the poll that asks") is unimplemented at the poll. (b)/(c) are not supported by the writes (recorded in BUG-070). *Fix shape (next stage, behind the existing `cdvd.delivery_visibility=poll`):* at the load-wait poll with no host-served read in flight, drain the IOP kernel up to the frame's ceiling without advancing the frame base (a `PumpWithinFrame` beside `PumpAfterEeService`), the module's `sceCdRead` ends taken from the ONE `DeliveryMeter` drive timeline (not `kCompletionUs`), the queued end-callbacks delivered at that poll; the lift yields when the IOP's next due lies past the frame's edge (the drive's rule). Pins: unit (a module completion inside the frame visible at the poll; one past the edge yields) + `RootCdvdPollVisibility` 1500 frames. *Probes:* unchanged from the P8.2-A review's numbers (no model change this stage) — poll b60 125/89, b120 433344/171; the +8,665 RAM0 words at b120 vs boundary are NOT yet attributed (next question: a poll-mode image diff at b120 — `verify/diff_legs`-style word census over the two `state_b120` images, both at hand: `p82a/poll1500/state_b120.ps2st` vs `fmvfix`/S2's boundary image). *Not measurable still:* the case-7 boundary, the title screen, `snd_kon`, W142, `iop_cdvd reads`, `cdvd_completions_per_frame` (blocked by BUG-070). *Tooling:* an lldb-launched child never leaves dyld in this sandbox (`stall1/stacks.txt`), and `lldb -p` attach is denied — the `--watch-flush-from` knob replaces the lldb flush helper; hot words (the SM's `+0x14/+0x18`) make the FMV wait burst take minutes under the watch (frames are not counted while parking: bound on the trace's growth). **Measured outcome: DP-17 stays OPEN; BUG-070 ATTRIBUTED (candidate (a), the IOP-progress clause at the poll), fix not yet landed.**

**DP-17 (a) — attempt-4 measurements, continued: BUG-070 DEMONSTRATED and FIXED (P8.4-A, 2026-10-03; `int_work/p84a/`; default still `boundary`, `boundary` bit-identical: `bnd200` five lanes IDENTICAL vs `fmvfix/det200`, the boundary probe images sha-identical to the P8.3-A review's, `RootPlayPolicyInert` in the full suite).** *Demonstrated first (the reviewer's MAJOR 2; `spin4/watch.log`, diag binary, 26 words incl. the loader SM `0xbb2560`'s head/count/cur, the Beach entry's counters, the slot table's byte 0, the STREAM latch/batch, with the new `--watch-record-from 620` (stores before a boundary are not recorded: the SM's hot words no longer fill the 20000-line cap) and the flush every 64 stores, the census complete at the kill):* frame 624 runs 9 host-served laps (window delivered → `FUN_002ad110` completes the fs → `FUN_002b4738` finishes the SM job → `FUN_002b4618` dequeues the next → it issues the next 22-byte window), then the SM pushes 41 jobs at the ring head and dequeues one whose body stages a type-0x7f MODULE-SERVED record (`FUN_002ad0b0` → cmds 28/41/14, `FUN_001e1500` slot 0 `0x0->0x2`) and SHIPS it inside the lap (`FUN_001e2958` latch 9); from then on every lap writes only the lap counters and SM2's empty dequeue: `FUN_002b4738` returns 1 while the SM is BUSY (cur/count/deferred ≠ 0), so the loop runs while the job's `isDone` (slot 0 == 0) is false — a wait on the module's service of that record, whose IOP work (the worker's `sceCdRead`, the DMA, the status reply through the end-callback `FUN_001e1f48`) NEW ran only at boundary slots 4/5. `iop_pending` at the spin: `events=0,rpc=1,due_in_frame=0` (the ship's end-callback queued, no CD event yet, the hard timer past the ceiling). The P8.3-A counter-evidence resolves: `iop_cdvd reads=124` at 121 and 624 because the record is staged after the last census, inside the spin frame; the 12-line census was the flush truncation. *Decisions MADE (one key, `cdvd.delivery_visibility=poll`):* (A) **nothing due → the frame ends.** `LoaderSpinLift::Decide` at the two engaged sites, when (g4) did not yield and no host read is in flight or landed at the poll, runs the owner's `PumpWithinFrame` (`IopModuleTable::PumpWithinFrame` = the slot-6 drain to the inclusive frame ceiling with NO frame-base advance; `EeRpcServer::Service`; `RpcRouter::CompletePending`) and answers "headroom" when the IOP RAN (a thread, a completion, a beat), "no headroom" (the lift, `idle`) when nothing is due inside the frame. Hardware rationale: the IOP runs on its own clock while the EE spins and the EE's next poll sees it; the console's lap loop ends by time. (A′) a delivered end-callback alone is NOT "headroom": measured first (`spin5`), counting it held the frame forever — with the record shipped, the title re-asks the module's status EVERY lap (`FUN_001e2958` → reply `1->0->1`: still in progress) while the module's read is due past the edge; on the console only time ends that, here the IOP's next due past the ceiling is that end and the boundary's pump completes the read (one frame per module read whose due crosses an edge). (B) **one drive:** `CdvdmanModule::SetCompletionDue` → `DeliveryMeter::ChargeReadAtMs` (seek from the head the EE's reads left + transfer at the poll scale); the issue instant is the domain's frame start + the IOP's offset in its frame and the end maps back onto the IOP clock — measured first too: the IOP clock's base is its boundary count (624 frames = 10.40 s) while the meter's timeline is the domain tick's (1856 fields = 30.9 s: parks and the FMV's demanded fields), so the naive mapping put the module's completion 20 s ahead (`spin5` `events=1,due_in_frame=0`). Boundary mode: the meter charges nothing, `kCompletionUs` stands. Rejected: a per-RPC service latency (a constant of ours to make the status ping-pong end by time — not needed once replies do not count); counting SM laps as emulated work (a constant; named below as the remedy if needed). *Measured (`poll1500/`, dev binary, `--frames 1500 --state-every 60`, 140 s):* `frames=1500 finalized=1 park=none`, `loader_spin_lifts=lap1=434,lap2=471,declines=4409,idle=1555,iop_pumps=100`, `cdvd_poll_deliveries=2879 cdvd_wait_deliveries=903 cdvd_wait_overruns=0 cdvd_wait_parks=198/198 cdvd_forced=0 cdvd_seeks=contig=4013,short=22,long=6`, `loader_reads=5/3782/12822157` (boundary at 1500: `2118`; poll 1.79×), `iop_cdvd=reads=259,sectors=453,callbacks=259` (OLD 334 before the edge; 124 before this fix), `iop_pending=events=0,rpc=0,due_in_frame=0` at the cap, `snd_kon=0`, `stream_rpc_calls=3063`. **Case 7 NOT reached by 1500**: boot case by image `b240:6 … b1440:6` (boundary the same); the title screen not reached (no `frames.fhash` line after 624 equals an OLD key ≥ 1010; the 876 lines after 624 are all distinct = the loading screen animating); W142 unchanged (first differing audio window at key 142 either way). The load PROGRESSES: Beach sub0 `0xc1aa20` state 3 → 4 by b1440, the SM ring 35 → 18 jobs, 2219 host windows after the former spin (6 per frame on the 369 frames that read; their drive time = 358 frames; the load took 876+). *The console estimate vs the model:* the drive would finish those reads in ≈ 6.0 s = 358 frames; the model takes > 876 frames because the pure-CPU laps (deserialising landed windows, the SM's job cycle) get ONE lap per frame once nothing is due — the console does ≈ 16 ms of them per frame. This under-count is the rule's accepted cost; the remedy, if the load must take plausibly many frames, is an EMULATED-WORK charge for the SM's lap (the EE's instant advancing by modelled work inside the frame — a later decision, named here, not a constant of ours now). *Probes (`probe_images.py` + `score.py --legs coldboot,artdet_f37152`):* poll coldboot b60 125/89 (unchanged), b120 **434692**/171 (P8.2-A: 433344; +1348 RAM0 words from the one-drive charge of the 124 FMV-phase module reads — the +8,665 vs boundary and this delta are NOT attributed to words; next question unchanged), artdet 64/98, 400/97 (poll images byte-identical to boundary's); boundary = S2 (12547/89, 424679/120; `outside_mask_words=438094`). Pins: `LoaderSpinLift` t14–t18, `CdvdmanModuleDisc` one-drive, `IopModuleTable` `PumpWithinFrame`, `RootCdvdPollVisibility` at 1500 frames (TIMEOUT 1800: ≈ 280 s). **Measured outcome: BUG-070 FIXED under the toggle; DP-17 stays OPEN — poll now outpaces boundary on reads (1.79×) and never stalls to 1500, but the case-7 edge, the title screen and W142 remain unreached at 1500 frames (the one-lap-per-frame under-count); the orchestrator scores; the named next decision is the emulated-work charge.**

**DP-17 (a) — attempt 4 continued per HW-1: the module round trip costs the IOP's modelled service time inside the frame; the lap continues on replies; case 7 REACHED (P8.5-A, 2026-10-03; `int_work/p85a/`; default still `boundary`, `boundary` bit-identical: `bnd200` five lanes IDENTICAL vs `fmvfix/det200`, the bridge's charge is 0 and the lift's new branch is inside `PollVisibility()` — `RootPlayPolicyInert` in the suite).** *Decision MADE (14:52): (A″) a STREAM.IRX round trip has a modelled cost on the frame's timeline.* HW-1 refutes P8.4-A's A′ ("a delivered reply alone is not headroom"): on the console a reply lets the title issue its next request within the same frame and ≈ 100 round trips fit (replies 48.0k–56.8k EE cycles apart in bursts). The model: every completed EE→IOP round trip through the `SifRpcBridge` (only STREAM.IRX is bridged; padman/mcserv/sdr are direct HLE faces) advances the IOP's virtual clock by the title's measured service time AFTER the body ran (the reply's instant) — `SifRpcBridge::SetServiceUs`, default 0 = today; the value is TITLE DATA with the HW-1 provenance (`LoaderSpinLift::Layout::stream_rpc_service_us = 160`: the low end of the 163–193 µs reply spacing at 294.912 MHz, the rest being the title's own work between replies), bound by the root under `cdvd.delivery_visibility=poll` only. The IOP's in-frame clock is thereby the EE's instant inside the frame (the one sub-frame quantity, modelled, emulated time only; `IopClock::OffsetInFrameUs`). At the lift's two sites a round trip completed since the previous decision (the bridge's ticket count moved) is noted at EVERY decision — it belongs to the lap that issued it, whatever ends that lap (review MINOR 2: the (g4) early return used to leave it to the next lap) — and, after (g4) did not yield, such a lap runs the owner's `PumpAtInstant` (`IopModuleTable::PumpToInstant` = the drain to the IOP's NOW, not to the ceiling — the module's drive completion fires once the instant passed it; the EE-side service; the queued end-callbacks → the reply visible to the title's next poll; NO frame-base advance) and answers "headroom" while the instant lies inside the slice (`rpc` laps: a reply IS progress), "no headroom" when the slice is consumed (`edge` lifts: the frame ends at the boundary, which moves the base past the overshoot < S, never backwards). A lap with no round trip keeps P8.4-A's rule (the pump to the ceiling; nothing due → `idle`). The round trips per frame FALL OUT of 16.7 ms / 160 µs minus the drive's gaps — no per-frame constant (measured below: 83 at most, 27 on average in the frames the rule decided in — the drive's gaps dominate). Rejected: charging the service in `SceSif::CallRpc` (not owned, and the IOP's clock is the bridge's domain); charging before the body (the handler's `sceCdRead` would be dated at the reply, not the issue); ordering the reply rule before (g4) (the console keeps polling while a host read is in flight past the edge, but without a round trip nothing advances the instant — (g4) stays the fallback; a round trip with a host read past the edge yields today, recorded as INT-P85A-G4-BEFORE-REPLY). *Measured (`poll1500/`, dev binary 14:46, `--frames 1500 --state-every 60`, deterministic, 151 s):* `frames=1500 finalized=1 park=none`, `loader_spin_lifts=lap1=671,lap2=829,declines=9559,idle=171,iop_pumps=0,rpc=3657,edge=93`, `stream_rpc_calls=6906` (P8.4-A 3063), `stream_rpc_service=160us,1104960`, `stream_rpc_per_frame=273,4.60` — **the 273 is NOT the reply rule's figure**: that census counts every STREAM RPC of every frame and its max falls in the FMV phase (the park loop's status pings; the BOUNDARY leg reads `266,2.05`, a 200-frame poll leg `273,2.25` with `rpc=1,edge=0`), and the 4.60 is the leg-wide mean; the rule's own measure is the census over the 173 frames in which the lift decided on a reply — `stream_rpc_per_lift_frame=83,26.90,173` (at most 83 module round trips in such a frame, 26.9 on average) and `stream_reply_laps_per_frame=83,21.67` (3657 `rpc` + 93 `edge` decisions) — against the model's ≈ 104: in the load the (g4) read-past-the-edge yield ends ≈ 1500 laps (`lap1=671,lap2=829`) while the slice's end (`edge`) ends 93, so most load frames end on the drive's next completion lying past the edge, not on the round trips' service time (INT-P85A-G4-BEFORE-REPLY is the mechanism; INT-P85A-REPLY-LAPS-UNDER-MODEL rows the 83/27-vs-104 gap); `iop_inframe_us=1044174,30681.35` (the max is the FMV phase's forced advances of the park loop — INT-P84A-TWO-CLOCKS, present under boundary too: `bnd200` 875624), `cdvd_completions_per_frame=901,3.58`, `loader_polls_per_frame=14,0.14`, `loader_reads=5/5375/15806007` (P8.4-A 3782; boundary 2118: 2.54×), `cdvd_poll_deliveries=4472 cdvd_wait_deliveries=903 cdvd_wait_overruns=0 cdvd_wait_parks=217/217 cdvd_forced=0 cdvd_seeks=contig=5629,short=78,long=6`, `iop_cdvd=reads=338,sectors=577,callbacks=338` (OLD 334 before the edge; P8.4-A 259). **Boot case by image: `b240:6 … b1080:6 b1140:7 … b1440:7` — the case 6 → 7 edge lies between b1080 and b1140, ≈ 840–900 frames after case 6 (the reference: 500–780; P8.4-A: not by 1500); the Beach load is complete at b1440 (`ram_dump`: beach `st=22`, both loader SMs `count=0 cur=0x0`).** NOT reached: the title-screen music — `snd_kon=0`, `snd_active=0`, all 250 audio windows silent (OLD non-silent from W142 = frame 852, its edge); `iop_cdvd reads` grew by only ≈ 80 after P8.4-A and the module streams nothing after the edge (INT-P85A-CASE7-SILENT: the next question — what the title asks the module at case 7 and why the module's worker (tid 2, `Wait/Sema`) does not stream; the `stream_rpc_cmds` census caps at 256 ids and hides the post-edge commands). W142 therefore unchanged (first differing audio window 142: NEW silent `0x0a6fb8af536edb25`, OLD `0x6e5c320f7c150766`); the title screen's frames cannot be matched by hash (OLD's 489 keys ≥ 1010 are all distinct — an animated screen; no `drawir` baseline exists) — reported by the case word. Probes below. Pins: `LoaderSpinLift` t19–t21 (a frame of 16,667 µs admits ⌊16667/160⌋ = 104 reply laps then the edge lift; the next frame continues on its first reply; a landed read iterates and a read past the edge yields before the reply rule; the lap ≥ 2 site; boundary never takes the branch), `IopModuleTable` `TestPumpToInstantFiresOnlyWhatThePresentPassed` (a due the instant passed fires, a later due waits, base/ceiling untouched, an overshoot carries into the next frame — BUG-066's pins green), `SifRpcBridge` `TestRoundTripServiceCharge` (default 0; 160 after the body; ten round trips = 1,600 µs; a passed due fires at `PumpToInstant`), `RootCdvdPollVisibility` (`rpc > 0 edge > 0`; `stream_rpc_per_lift_frame` max ≥ 50 — the discriminating census, a build without the rule reads `0,0.00,0`; `stream_reply_laps_per_frame` max ≥ 2; boundary `stream_rpc_service=0us,0 rpc=0 edge=0 stream_rpc_per_lift_frame=0,0.00,0`; `stream_rpc_per_frame` is reported, never asserted — the review's MAJOR 1 replaced the first version's `stream_rpc_per_frame max ≥ 50`, which the FMV phase satisfied with no reply rule at all), t20's carry-over case (a round trip of the lap (g4) ended is not the next lap's: red before). **Measured outcome: landed behind the toggle; `poll` now reaches boot case 7 (the title screen) inside 1500 frames with 2.54× boundary's reads and no stall; the music after the edge is the remaining NEW gap; the orchestrator scores; not KEPT by this stage.**

**DP-18 — DECISION MADE:** downstream of DP-19/DP-17 (the f57292/f59991 stall walks the visible-group list that only NEW's progressing scene
rotates). Re-evaluated after gate run 5 with DP-17 in force; if the stall persists it is a NEW defect in the walked list (an infinite walk
hardware would not do) — up to three attempts, then OPEN.
**Status (P8.7-A, 2026-10-03; `int_work/p87a/`): the wrong word is NAMED and it is not in the walked list — it is the main thread's `$sp`.** On the 885a29b dev binary f57292 still stops at 15 `frames.fhash` lines; lldb attached to the stalled leg (`att_regs.py`/`att_sp.py`) shows `FUN_0027edf0`'s frame at `sp = 0x6ffffdc0` (`EDF0 sp=0x6ffffdc0 a0=0xe2b410 a1=0xdc3980 a2=0x50 ra=0x2820b8`), BELOW the scratchpad: its locals `[sp+0x5c]` (group-table base), `[sp+0x64]` (outer index) live at `0x6ffffe1c/0x6ffffe24`, which `AddressMap::Translate` maps to nothing — the store is dropped and the load returns 0 (`r3 = 0x3d0 = 0 + 0x7a*8` at line 10398; `i` reads back 0 → `i+1 = 1 < 3` forever). The tables are intact (`walk_dump.py`: count word `0x309e6c = 3`, list `0x7a,0x7b,0xba`, groups 26/25/25 entries; the `0x3d9ae0` rotation of I8.9-B is a symptom of the camera, not the hang). *The writer:* the sp was balanced after the relaunch (`FUN_0017ce50` inner sp `0x70004270` in frame 0) and dropped 0x32b0 during frame 1 inside `FUN_001990b8`'s virtual update `FUN_0022f758` of objects `0xde7d30`/`0xde7880` (−0x1f50, −0x1360) → `FUN_0020c020 → FUN_0020f6a8 → FUN_00206770 → FUN_00115318 → FUN_00242868`, whose loop calls `0x178470` and loses exactly 0x10 per call (`sp_trace_FUN_00242868.txt`: `sp=0x70003f70 → 3f60 → 3f50 → 3f40 …`). Cause: NEW's pure-discovery generation split the retail function `0x178470–0x178774` at its own branch delay slot — `FUN_00178470` (`lhu; andi; bne; addiu sp,-0x10`, then a tail call to `0x178480`/`0x17859c`) + `FUN_0017847c` whose `Label_0000` IS the delay-slot `addiu sp,-0x10` and whose switch has no `case 0x178480`, so the fall-through entry re-runs the prologue; one epilogue → −0x10 net. OLD's Ghidra-fed shard has ONE function with `case 0x178480: goto Label_0001` (`OLD/build/recompiled_shard_008.cpp`), hence no leak and no stall. f59991's depths (`0x70001cb0` first call, `0x700009e0` at the stall vs the balanced `0x70003070`) are the same leak accumulating until the walk's frame crosses `0x70000000` (≈ frame 126). **BUG-072** filed. Not a construction gap in an owned module: the fix is in the generated TUs' generator/discovery (not owned) → `RESULT: BLOCKED`; pins named in BUG-072. Attempt count for DP-18: 1 of 3 (attribution; no fix attempted).

**DP-18 — DECISION MADE (P8.9-A, 2026-10-03; `int_work/p89a/`): fixed by REGENERATION with the delay-slot rule in NEW's recompiler; measured.** *Evidence:* BUG-072 (the status above). *Options:* (a) discovery rule — a weak seed (prologue pattern / data pointer / code pointer) whose address is the delay slot of the preceding branch/jump is never a function start (the slot executes WITH its transfer, on both paths; a function cannot begin there); plus leader completeness — every address flow enters from outside a function (branch fall-through, jal/jalr return site, external jump-table target) is a leader of the function that CONTAINS it, so its `switch (addr)` carries the case; (b) hand-add `case 0x178480` (forbidden: the shards are never hand-edited, and it leaves the class alive at the other 11 sites); (c) a runtime guard in `find_containing_function` (symptom-level). *Choice:* (a), the MIPS rule itself, implemented in `tools/recompiler_v2/recompiler_tool/discovery/functions.cpp` (`Walker::in_delay_slot`, `eligible`, the leader sweep) + `discovery_types.h` (diagnostic sets) + `main_v2.cpp` (report line); a STRONG seed in a delay slot (jal / table / ELF entry) is KEPT as an entry — the hardware does start executing there when called — and reported (`[v2]   STRONG ENTRY IN DELAY SLOT: 0x002d2180 (preceding transfer at 0x002d217c)`: the word before is a stray `jr ra` padding word after the previous function's `jr ra; nop`, never executed; the entry is a real prologue — the report is informational). Game-agnostic (design §6): no addresses in `discovery/`. *Toggle:* `tools/regen_from_frozen.sh` default = NEW's built tool; `--frozen-old` regenerates the pre-fix set from OLD's binary for proofs. *Measured:* report `[v2] discovered 8807 functions, 177 jump-table sites, 8848 unreached gap words` (8819 − 12; the SAME 8848 gap words — no code lost), `[v2] delay-slot rule: 12 weak seed(s) rejected, 1 strong entry in a delay slot`; three bounded runs byte-identical (`diff -r a b is EMPTY (34 emitted files)`); function-level diff vs the frozen shards with the dense `recomp_call` ids normalised: `removed=12 added=0 changed=30 identical=8777` — the 12 removed (`FUN_00127224 FUN_00128a58 FUN_0017847c FUN_001c8024 FUN_001c878c FUN_00221b98 FUN_00226d04 FUN_0022b8ec FUN_0022b954 FUN_0022b9bc FUN_002409d8 FUN_0025196c`) are exactly the delay-slot starts, the 12 containers (`FUN_00127200 FUN_00128a38 FUN_00178470 FUN_001c8020 FUN_001c8788 FUN_00221b88 FUN_00226cf8 FUN_0022b8d8 FUN_0022b940 FUN_0022b9a8 FUN_002409d0 FUN_00251960`) now hold the whole function (e.g. `FUN_00178470`: 39 → 543 lines, `case 0x178480: goto Label_0001`), the other 18 are 9 shard-boundary pairs differing by one blank line (the 32-way partition shifted by one function at 9 boundaries); everything else differs only in the dense id operand of `recomp_call(<id>u, <addr>u, …)` (the address operand unchanged). Pins: `Bug072SpBalance` (RED on the frozen shards: `sp delta … fall-through … : -16`, `summed over 10 … : -160`; GREEN after: `0`, `0`), `RecompDiscoveryDelaySlotRule` (synthetic fixture), `RootAnchorF57292` / `RootAnchorF59991` (`--frames 600`: both `frames=600 finalized=1 park=none restore_mhash_ok=1`, ≈70 s each; before: 15 / ≈126 frames). **KEPT.** Attempt count for DP-18: 2 of 3 (one fix attempt, kept).

**DP-20 — DECISION MADE (2026-10-02 17:50): (a) + (c)**, the user's order. (c): a restored RUNNING hard timer is due at the restore (first beat

(default `at_restore` once KEPT); hardware rationale: a timer mid-period at the save has at most one period left, the earliest admissible phase.

BUG-066), so OLD never releases finished voices after a restore; NEW runs the retail 16727 µs period. The anchors' audio windows from the

**DP-20 (c) — IMPLEMENTED behind the toggle (I8.13-A, 2026-10-02; default OFF = today's behaviour until the gate KEEPs it).** Toggle: Config
`iop.restored_timer_due = one_period | at_restore` (default `one_period`; `--set iop.restored_timer_due=at_restore`). Model
(`engine/iop/hle/timrman/timrman_module.{h,cpp}`, `engine/iop/iop_kernel.{h,cpp}`): `TimrmanModule::RestoreHardTimer` arms the restored RUNNING
timer through the new `IopKernel::StartPeriodicTimerAtUs(first_due, period, beat)` with `first_due = now` under `at_restore` (the handler runs in
the FIRST `Dispatch` after the restore, before any RPC-woken thread of that pass: beat → release pass → sweep → KOFF with the restored ENDX
latch) and `now + period` under `one_period` (today; the first boundary pump's inclusive ceiling reaches it after the EE's RPC passes). The rule
is bound by the root (`stream_irx.Timrman().SetRestoredTimerDue(cfg.iop.restored_timer_due)`) before any restore; no savestate-format change.
Census `iop_restored_timer_due=`. Pins: `IopKernelFacingModules` (`TestTimrmanRestoredDue`: after `RebaseToFrame(40304)` + restore, `at_restore`
beats in the first Dispatch, `one_period` not before the first boundary pump) + root `RootAnchorHeartbeatAtRestore` (slowspot_f40298 under the
toggle: `iop_hb_beats >= frames`, `state_b1` v29 phase Off — I8.8-A's dropped check re-enabled); `RootAnchorHeartbeat` pins the default.

**DP-21 — DECISION MADE (2026-10-02 17:50): (a) first** — the operand capture on both sides (OLD `[VCALLMS-PROBE]`, NEW a diag `Vu0Registry`
VF-in/out dump per `vcallms 0x790`), then: operands equal → the op is adjudicated against the PS2 rule (PCSX2's VU interpreter semantics in
