## DP-31 — The META-ARENA hook (HOOK row `0x181560`): OLD routed the title's handle-heap nodes to a host arena at `0x02000000` to hide a host-side free-list corruption; the console keeps them on the title's page pool (slab class 7) — the hook is OLD's defect, not the title's; NEW's model needs no routing, the arena stays read-only storage for OLD-written images; `hle.meta_arena` toggle, pinned against the console, scored under DT-7 (2026-10-06, P8.32-A)

**Why the hook exists (OLD's own words, read-only).** `OLD/host_app/main.cpp:397-415`: "[META-ARENA] Option-A root fix: dedicated arena for the handle allocator's metadata/descriptor nodes (FUN_00181560(<=0x20) allocations from the pool-allocator code range [0x202000,0x206000)). These nodes are normally taken from the shared game slab (region 1); when one is freed its slab memory was reused as game data while pool 0x31b138 still referenced it, stomping the free-list with region-1 (key-buffer) pointers -> the case-6 key overwrite. Routing them to a separate EE region (0x02000000 …) makes that reuse impossible." `main.cpp:3186-3256` installs the two `install_dual_hook`s (`0x181560` alloc: `v0 = hle_meta_arena::Alloc()`, `pc = ra`, the slab body skipped; `0x181590` free: arena pointers freed in the arena). `hle_meta_arena.h:16-28` names the mechanism it hid: "A descriptor freed while anything still references it (a stale link, a stale job pointer, an op node in flight) turns those walks into traversals of REUSED memory … the measured 20-70ms 'pick' calls … plus the historical [POOL-FREELIST-WATCH] 0x5-poisoned links and [HANDLE-WILD-ALLOC] wild buffers". HOOK_RECLASSIFICATION §3.1 row `0x181560` classifies the defect as HOST-INDUCED: "the corruption does not occur on retail/PCSX2, so an upstream host divergence (the boot double-init leak family …, and/or the loader delivery divergence at 0x2ad110) poisons the free list … the arena is itself a non-hardware invention". NEW inherited the hook verbatim as a HOOK row (`games/<title>/hle/workarounds/meta_arena.{h,cpp}`, `free_list_0x181560.{h,cpp}`; retirement gate in the header: "when the boot/level legs run with the arena disabled and mem.mhash/frames.fhash still match OLD's baselines" — a gate that can never close, since OLD's baselines CARRY the arena).

**Classification: (a) an OLD-only host defect.** The title's own code (`FUN_00181560` = `FUN_002044f8(FUN_00181620(), size)`, the page-pool allocator; `FUN_00181590` = `FUN_002045a8(pool, ptr)`) keeps the descriptors on the page pool, and so does the hardware: PCSX2 at the matched moment (`int_work/p828a/pcsx2_rec159_ee.bin`, `0x323278 = 0x159`, boot-SM case 2) holds `desc in RAM 385 / META 0`, every descriptor a USED chunk of slab class 7/32 B (`{'cls 7/32B': 385}`), 12 class-7 pages `[6, 43, 44, 45, 48, 55, 62, 119, 131, 132, 141, 150]`, the chain head `+0x50 = 0xb92700` (P8.29-B `INT-P829B-CONSOLE-DESCRIPTORS-ON-SLAB`; re-measured `int_work/p832a/desc_walk.out`). No overwrite pattern is in the console's class-7 pages (the walk honours every allocator invariant: free-flagged == free-list reach, +0x4c == free count, used == the live counter). The corruption OLD hid was OLD's: NEW does not reproduce it with the routing off (below: a 121-frame coldboot and a 61-frame resume of OLD's `old_b120` both finalize with every invariant held). Not (b): the hook masks no title write.

**Options.** (a) remove the routing (the title's allocator runs as recompiled — what the hardware does), keep the arena's storage read-only for images that already hold nodes there; (b) keep the routing (OLD's observable). **Choice: (a) behind the toggle**, default (b) until the score keeps (a).

**Toggle.** Config `hle.meta_arena` (`engine/core/config/config.{h,cpp}` `HleConfig::meta_arena`, `tools/gen_settings_table.py` ROW, `docs/filemap/settings_table.tsv` 67 rows, PHASE1_PLAN §3; default `on`). `off`: `free_list_0x181560::InstallHooks(…, route_allocations=false)` installs ONLY the free body (`0x181590`) — `FUN_00181560` runs as recompiled, every node lands on the page pool; the free body falls through for every non-arena pointer (inert on a fresh boot: `meta_arena=off,hooks=1,allocs=0,frees=0`) and frees arena pointers in the arena, which a restored OLD-written image still holds (the META section restores their bytes, the HEAP tail their bookkeeping; `FUN_002045a8(pool, 0x200xxxx)` would otherwise treat an arena pointer as a page of the pool). The HEAP section's wire layout is unchanged: with `off` a fresh run's tail is the empty arena (capacity, all-zero states, bump 0); OLD-written tails decode as before. Removing a hook is the point; no hook was added (the retained free body is the existing one, arena-pointers-only). run.meta `meta_arena=<on|off>,hooks=,allocs=,frees=,live=,bump=`.

**Pins.** `WorkaroundFreeListHook` (+1 check: `route_allocations=false installs only the free body (0x181590), never the alloc site`); `Coretest_config` (`hle.meta_arena` default on, `Set("off")` round-trips); `RootMetaArenaOff` (`tests/app/root_meta_arena_off.py`, TIMEOUT 300, SKIP 77): coldboot 121 frames `--set hle.meta_arena=off`, the b120 image walked with the title's layouts against the console image — GREEN (`int_work/p832a/pin_off.log`: `PASS descriptors: NEW RAM 385 / META 0 == console RAM 385 / META 0`, `PASS every descriptor a USED cls 7/32B chunk on both`, `PASS class-7 page count NEW 12 == console 12`, `PASS the console's class-7 pages before the first divergent pop are NEW's: [6, 43, 44, 45, 48, 55, 62] (first divergent pop: page 106, console cls 56 / NEW cls 52)`), RED with `--arm on` (`pin_on.log`: `FAILED: meta_arena,desc_location,desc_class,cls7_pages` — `desc in RAM 0 / META 385`, 9 pages). Restore with `off` (OLD's `old_b120.ps2st` resumed 61 frames, debug): `finalized=1 frames=61 heap_section_restored=1 meta_arena_bump_restored=644 meta_arena=off,hooks=1,…,live=356`; the saved b60 walks with every invariant: `desc in RAM 31 / META 355` (MIXED: the restored nodes in META, the new ones on class 7); first frame hash = the lane's `0x91112b3860dce5fc`. `RootColdbootProbeResumes` runs at the default and is unchanged.

**Console compare with `off` (`int_work/p832a/{desc_walk,page_diff}.out`, `compare_off.txt`).** Pages 0–105 of the page pool agree class for class between the console and NEW(off) (with `on`: the same prefix, class 7 short by 3 pages). The FIRST page pop whose class differs is page 106: console class 56/576 B, NEW class 52/368 B — the console holds 18 class-56 pages vs NEW's 16 (`+106, +108`), 8 class-48/272 B vs 7 (`+107`), 2 class-36/148 B vs 1 (`+147`); NEW holds 4 class-26/108 B vs 3 (`+165`): in-use pages console 169 / NEW off 166 / NEW on 163. That is the class P8.29-B first called `slab-old-held-new-freed` and the sibling stage P8.32-B's census re-classed (LEDGER P8.32-B (4)): NOT a NEW free — objects the hardware builds by record 0x36 (`FUN_00178388 → … → FUN_001a8268`; 20 registry objects, the 576 B tables among them) that NEW never builds through b240, a NEW construction-gap candidate (console≡OLD; P8.33-A's BUG-089 names the type registry's missing entries); it shifts every later pop by −3 pages, so the console's later class-7 pages `119/131/132/141/150` are NEW's `116/128/129/138/146`. The P8.29-B partition of the 58 pages whose class differs OLD vs NEW re-run with `off`: `{'con==new': 4, 'con==old': 30, 'con-neither': 23, 'con-free': 1}` words `{2876, 17703, 16767, 47}` (with `on`: `10 / 28 / 19 / 1`, `7751 / 15957 / 13155 / 47`) — the page-assignment class now has ONE named cause left (the pops of the objects NEW never builds), no longer two.

**Row.** `P8-META-ARENA-OLD-HOOK` FILED (EQUIVALENCE_PLAN §7.1) under DT-7's rule: the OLD-side cause is named by OLD's own code and comment (quoted above), the console proof is the image at the matched pair. Directive: nine exact ROW rules in `verify/attribution/classes.tsv` (the pool header `0x31b138-0x31b190`, the handle table `0x31b190-0x31b700`, the seven shared class-7 pages), `recorded:P8.32-A proof=console:int_work/p828a/pcsx2_rec159_ee.bin@coldboot:b120`, leg `coldboot`; they precede the OPEN record-pool rules. Booked ONLY where console == NEW ≠ OLD. **Found while filing:** `verify/relocate_compare.walk_pool` walks the record pool's descriptors in META only (`META_BASE <= d`, `img.mw`), so `attribute_probe --reloc` FAILED on an `off` image (`pool walk: record buf 0x0 outside pool 0xf59ac0+0x10a3d40 (desc 0x200004c)`). **Corrected in review loop 1 (the reviewer's BLOCKER):** the gate's booking path IS affected whenever a proved `@reloc-class` directive exists for the pair — since 04:16 the sibling P8.32-B's two directives (`unmatched-record` / `unpaired-record`, `coldboot:b120`) send `booked_console_only` through `run(…, reloc=True)` = the structural walk, which raised on the `off` b120 image; the 05:00 gate (launched with that file, the stage's record claiming the path unaffected) could never have reached its score. Fixed in this loop: `verify/relocate_compare.walk_pool` follows the pool header's chain wherever it points (RAM0 page-pool chunks on the hardware / `off`, the META section under the workaround, both in a restored image), each side from its OWN `+0x50` head (attribute_probe hands over OLD's head; the override applies only when it is not simply OLD's header word); selftest on the META shape, the RAM shape, the MIXED shape and a dangling link (`VerifyAttributeRelocateSelftest` PASS); the full `--reloc` ledger on the `off` b120 image now walks (`int_work/p832a/attr_off_reloc_loop1.run.log`: OLD `desc_in_meta 385`, NEW `desc_in_ram 385`).

**Preview of the decision on the b120 pair (debug images, `attribute_probe` without `--reloc`, `int_work/p832a/attr_{on,off}.run.log`):** `on` (= S12's binary behaviour) `434841 differing words … DT-7: booked_console=19538` (the ring row's 19,470 + 68 booked by the new rules — the class-7 pages hold relocation words at `on` too: 1,430 OLD≠NEW, 68 console-confirmed); `off` `440196 … booked_console=23839` (proof rows 24,990; console == OLD 15; console == neither 1,136). Unbooked: `on` 415,303, `off` 416,357 → `off` WORSE by 1,054 words on this pair: the 1,136 unbooked words of the row's ranges are descriptor link words (+0x10/+0x14/+0x18) and handle-table pointers into the −3-shifted later pages, and the MIXED growth (+1,573) is those later pages' descriptors — all the old-held-new-freed class's consequence, not the hook's. The anchors' pairs (OLD-written images: nodes in META; with `off` every new node goes to the slab while OLD's go to META; no console image for those pairs → nothing bookable) can only add. **Expected outcome: REVERT (default stays `on`) by the unbooked rule; the full 13-leg 3-rep gate decides** — `gate_run.py --release int_work/p832a/bin/nativeee_release --debug int_work/p832a/bin/nativeee_debug --out int_work/p832a/gate --reps 3 --pairing run --set hle.meta_arena=off --classes verify/attribution/classes.tsv` vs `int_work/gates/s12/score.json` (`score.py --compare`); the status line below carries the measured tuples.

**S12 (the reference gate, `hle.meta_arena=on`, `int_work/gates/s12/score.log`):** `tuple=(0, 20084447, 448)`, `tuple_unbooked=(0, 20064909, 448)` (`booked_console_words=19538`, exact proved rules only). **Comparability (corrected in review loop 1 — the reviewer's MAJOR):** S12's probe stage (04:15) read the tree's `classes.tsv` BEFORE the sibling P8.32-B's additions (04:16: two proved `@reloc-class` directives and the `PTC-HASHMAP` rule, coldboot b120), so a gate reading today's file would not have shared S12's rule set — the earlier claim "the two gates use the same file" was false for the tree the 05:00 gate read. Fixed by ONE classes snapshot for both sides, `int_work/p832a/classes_snapshot.tsv` (md5 `b0b5c2366a880a552a878b752cec0bca` = the tree's file at 05:34: the sibling's directives + the nine META-ARENA rules ACTIVE): S12's coldboot b120 pair re-booked on a deterministic `on` image produced with S12's own binary (`int_work/gates/bin_s12/nativeee_release`, `int_work/p832a/s12_rebook/probes/coldboot/new_b120.ps2st`, raw `434841` = S12's pair exactly) → `booked_console 41676 (full ledger with the structural walk)` by class `SECOND-RING 19470, UNMATCHED-RECORD 11403, UNPAIRED-RECORD 10474, PTC-HASHMAP 261, SLAB-CLASS7-DESCRIPTOR-PAGE 67, RECORD-POOL-HEADER 1` (the last two = the 68 words the META rules misattribute at `on`; they favour `on` by 68 words at most), S12 re-scored over its own battery dir (`int_work/p832a/s12_rebook/score.json`): `tuple_unbooked=(0, 20042771, 448)`. The `off` side: the 05:00 gate's guard (`guard=IDENTICAL`) and 13-leg 3-rep battery (complete 05:32, `META-OK 13/13 legs`) were kept; its probe + score stages were re-run by `int_work/p832a/probe_score.py` (gate_run.py's steps 3–4 verbatim) with the fixed walker and the snapshot (the gate process itself was stopped before its probe stage — its in-memory walker was the META-only one).

**Measured (review loop 1, `int_work/p832a/compare_loop1.txt`):**
```
compare: A=(0, 20084447, 448) B=(0, 20134958, 449) -> WORSE  [REPORTED: raw outside-mask words, Section 6]
compare (DT-7, unbooked): A=(0, 20042771, 448) B=(0, 20088981, 449) -> WORSE  [DECISION: raw minus the console-proved booked words; booked A=41676 B=45977]
decision: WORSE
```
`off` is WORSE by 46,210 unbooked words (raw +50,511), `legs_identical` 0/13 both, pins 449 ≥ 448. Per pair (`off` − `on`, every anchor pair identical): coldboot b60 +4,495 (raw 6,047 vs 1,552; no console image at b60 → nothing bookable; not attributed per word in this loop), coldboot b120 +1,054 (raw 440,196 vs 434,841; booked 45,977 vs 41,676 — the META row books 4,369 at `off`: 4,314 class-7-page + 53 handle-table + 2 header words; the unbooked residual = 1,136 `console_ne_new` words of the row's ranges = descriptor links / handle-table pointers into the −3-shifted later pages + those pages' descriptors), coldboot_attract b1500/2700/3780/4200/5400 +8,280/+8,173/+8,175/+8,001/+8,032 (= 40,661; the same scene edge as S12, `old_b850=new_b1048, offset +198`; no console image for those pairs → nothing bookable).

**Status (P8.32-A, review loop 1): REVERTED.** The default stays `hle.meta_arena=on` (OLD's observable); the toggle, the pins (`RootMetaArenaOff` GREEN `off` / RED `on` vs the console), the row (FILED) and the console evidence stay. The nine `classes.tsv` rules are INACTIVE (commented out) at the default — at `on` they book 68 relocation words the row's cause does not cover; the snapshot that scored `off` keeps them active. **The residual is not the hook's:** every `off` word the console can judge books (the row's ranges hold 5,520 differing words at b120: 4,369 booked, 15 where the console holds OLD's word, 1,136 where it holds neither — links and handle-table pointers into the −3-shifted later pages), and what remains — the page-106 shift — is the type-registry gap of LEDGER P8.32-B (4) (objects the hardware builds by record 0x36 and NEW never builds), being fixed by the sibling P8.33-A as BUG-089. **This decision is to be RE-SCORED after that fix lands** (the same command: the `off` gate vs the then-current reference under one classes snapshot with the nine rules active); the toggle is kept for it. Expected then: the b120 residual falls with the shift; the b60 and attract residuals (unbookable today, no console image at those moments) need a console image at a later moment or the DP-20 route to be judged.

**Status (P8.33-A, 2026-10-06): the type-registry gap is ATTRIBUTED and FIXED behind a toggle — BUG-089, not a model but an HLE calling-convention defect; the fix arm is NOT yet the default (below).** The loop that builds the registry (`FUN_00164ae8`, under `FUN_001700d8`) iterates the MC manager's slot-count nibble (`*(*(gp-0x7dcc)+0x14) & 0xf`, built as 4 by `FUN_002a2700`); NEW's `LibmcBindings::GetInfo` read `sceMcGetInfo`'s fifth argument `*format` from `sp+0x10` (o32) instead of `t0` (the EE convention; the title's call site `0x2a6d28` `addiu t0,a2,8`) and stored `format = 1` into that header word (`leg_diag/watch.log`: `FUN_002a2700+0xd9c:0x0->0xe004` then the host writer `0xe004->0x1`, b59) — one iteration instead of four: 8 of the console's 28 entries, 114 vs 126 class-22 objects. Fixed in `engine/mc/hle/libmc_bindings.cpp` (`format_ptr = A(ctx, 4)`) behind Config `hle.libmc_format_in_t0` (default `off` = the inherited stack read), pins `LibMcSaveFlow` (both arms) and `RootTypeRegistryComplete` (`--arm off` RED: nibble 1 / 19 slots (8 of the registry's) / 114; `--arm on` GREEN: 4 / 28 / 126 vs `pcsx2_rec159_ee.bin`). **Why `on` is not the default yet (DP-32 below, OPEN):** with `on` the full suite turned six Root pins red — the title's boot flow no longer reaches the FMV within 400 frames (`fmv inits=0`, `fmv_pictures_served=0`; `sceMcGetInfo` once per frame from b59) — the hardware plays the FMV with the slot count 4, so NEW's libmc model has a second gap the 4-slot flow exposes (candidates, unmeasured: the model answers a formatted PS2 card on all four (port, slot) pairs where PCSX2 has cards in port 0/1 slot 0 only and `-10 no card` elsewhere; the first-probe completion latency (30 frames on hardware); the single pending completion vs four per-slot pollers). **The re-score DP-31 asked for is now possible:** with the fix, `hle.meta_arena=off` at coldboot b120 agrees with the console on EVERY one of its 169 in-use pages class for class (`int_work/p833a/page_diff.out`: `con vs off: in-use pages con 169 / off 170; pages whose class differs: 1, first by index: 169 (con cls None, off cls 26)` — one extra NEW page beyond the console's set, a class-26/108 B page; was `first by index: 106`, 59 pages), while `on` differs on 36 pages from page 119 (`con cls 7, on cls 8` — the routing's class-7 shortfall alone). The DP-31 re-score (the `off` gate vs the then-current reference under one classes snapshot with the nine rules active) is the next P8.32-A step; this stage's own gate (the fix at the default `on`) is in `int_work/p833a/gate/` (see its SECTION).

**HW-1 — Reference measurements on PCSX2 (the fork with the DebugServer, `pcsx2-src/build/pcsx2-qt/PCSX2.app`, `-fastboot`, retail ISO; orchestrator, 2026-10-03 14:15–14:50).**
(1) Boot timeline (frames from the fast-boot reset; the ELF starts ≈ frame 240): boot SM word `0x98c140` (case = `(u64 >> 44) & 0x3f`) = 1 from ≈ 360, **5 at ≈ 1380–1860 (the movies play in real time: ≈ 1100 frames)**,
**6 (the Beach hub load) by ≈ 2160, 7 (the title screen) between ≈ 2640 and 2940 → the Beach load takes ≈ 500–780 frames on the reference** (NEW's deterministic poll leg: case 6 at b240, still 6 at b1440). (2) In the load, the title's
record counter `0xbb25b8` advances ≈ 731/frame (boot case 6) and ≈ 363/frame (the `loading_screen_start_gate_hunt.p2s` level load) while `FUN_002aa8d0` is entered ONCE per frame (breakpoint: consecutive hits 4,920,1xx cycles ≈ one
frame apart) — the counter counts records visited inside the poll, not laps; the title's loop runs one lap per frame on the console too. (3) **The STREAM reply processor `FUN_001e1f48` is hit in BURSTS: consecutive replies 48.0k–56.8k EE
cycles apart (≈ 163–190 µs = one SIF RPC round trip on the reference), with gaps of ≈ 0.88 frame between bursts (the module waiting on the drive)** — the title issues many module round trips per frame (≈ 100 fit in a frame) and each reply
lets it issue the next request; NEW's P8.4-A rule ("a delivered reply alone is not headroom"; yield when nothing is due) caps the loop at ONE round trip per frame — the under-count that makes the Beach load > 1500 frames. Decision → DP-17
attempt 4 (continued, P8.5-A): an RPC round trip costs the IOP's modelled service time on the frame's timeline (reference ≈ 160 µs), the lap continues while the IOP's in-frame time is inside the slice, and yields when the next modelled event
(RPC service completion / drive completion) falls past the frame edge — no per-frame constant, no OLD rate; the round-trips-per-frame fall out (≈ 100 by the model; P8.5-A measured 83 at most and 27 on average in the frames the rule decided in — the drive's completions past the edge end most load frames first).

**DP-17 attempt 4 — KEPT (orchestrator, 2026-10-03 19:10).** Gate S4 (`cdvd.delivery_visibility=poll` on the P8.5-A binary, Release, 3 reps, `docs/phases/gates/S4_2026-10-03_poll-p85a.md`):
`(0, 8935538, -1)` vs S2 `(0, 8939244, -1)` → BETTER (−3,706 probe words: coldboot b60 12547 → 125 — the DP-3 class is RESOLVED by poll visibility: both trees now consume Hub01.psm by boundary 60;
b120 424679 → 433344 (+8,665, the load's faster progress — attributed next); anchors ±10). 10/12 legs run as before (DP-18). The default is flipped to `poll` (`config.h`, PHASE1_PLAN §3 row,
settings table; the unit tests of the boundary model pin `Boundary` explicitly; `RootPlayPolicyInert`'s fixture re-recorded under the new default (its purpose — play-policy inertness — unchanged);
`RootHeadroomQueryRecompiled` now attributes every Demand tick to a wait source (FMV `park_ticks` + disc parked frames) instead of asserting zero; `RootCdvdPollVisibility`'s control leg names
`boundary` explicitly). BUG-071 (P8.6-A: retail's blocking `sceSifCallRpc` runs the end function before the waiter resumes; NEW deferred it to slot 4) LANDED under poll: the title-screen music
plays (`snd_kon=8`, first non-silent window W191 vs OLD's W142 — the edge is at b1134 vs OLD's b852; content vs OLD from the onset: 0/20 windows identical → INT-P86A-ONSET-CONTENT, next).

## LEDGER — the residual attribution ledger over gate S6's 24 probe pairs (P8.11-B first cut; MADE TRUSTWORTHY by P8.12-B, 2026-10-04; `int_work/p812b/ledger/`, `int_work/p812b/census/`; the CURRENT table and the structural attribution are P8.14-B's sub-section, C3 corrected and the AUDIO LEDGER started in P8.15-B's; the slab classes by allocation census, the AUDIO LEDGER by audibility (every verdict OPEN) and the per-window VERDICT TABLE are P8.17-B's sub-section; the tools corrected per its review, the page-init attribution, the audio ORDER class and the FINAL tables are P8.18-B's sub-section at the end of this section; P8.13-B's precedes them)

**What P8.12-B changed (the P8.11-B review's 1 BLOCKER + 4 MAJOR):** (1) the record-pool walk honours the allocator's own state — flags bit 0 = FREE must equal the set reachable from the 16 free-list heads (`pool+0xc..+0x48` via `+0x10`) and the header's `+0x4c` free count; bit 2 = PENDING (released, not yet free-listed — NEW b120 carries two: `0x1031f80` 0xb40 and `0x1032ac0` 0x500, flags `0x24006`, handle idx `0xfffffffe`); used (neither) must equal the live-record counter `0x323278` — asserted on both images (`OLD 385 descriptors (used 345, free 40, pending 0, counter 345) | NEW 385 (used 345, free 38, pending 2, counter 345)`); free/pending chunks are FREE units, never matched. (2) Pool records are matched FIRST by the allocator's own key (handle idx + size + bytes equal under relocation: 341 idx pairs, 232 equal), THEN the leftovers by content (`rehandled` = the same bytes under another handle: 102 records); same idx with another size = UNMATCHED (both-sided), what no pass pairs = UNPAIRED (10/10). (3) Every word is classed from BOTH sides (OLD[x] found at its relocated place AND NEW[x] a relocated copy); the side-status matrix is in `reloc_coldboot_b120.txt`. (4) No relocation class is booked to a row by the tool: the mapping is the TSV's `@reloc-class` directive and there is NONE — every relocation class is OPEN until the pool-side cause is named. (5) ROW rules wider than 64 words need a `recorded:<record>` token (else demoted to MIXED and reported); EE-VOICE-TABLE / STREAM-REPLY-BLOCK / PAGE-POOL-DESCRIPTORS / RECORD-POOL-HEADER+COUNT are no longer ROW; T0-DERIVED is the 17 recorded words. (6) The UNATTRIBUTED lists are complete (`ledger/<leg>_b<N>.unattributed.txt`, the JSON carries all) and the `--watch-word` writer census ran over every anchor's UNATTRIBUTED words (11 Debug diag legs, `--frames 61`, `census/<leg>/watch.log`): 1,008 of 1,142 (leg, word) pairs got a writer `FUN_+off`; 134 had no CPU store in the window (inherited from the restore).

**Instrument:** `verify/attribute_probe.py` (rules `verify/attribution/classes.tsv`, order: exact ROW/DP/OPEN rule (writer-gated where a census exists) → `verify/relocate_compare.py`'s both-sided class via `@reloc-class` → MIXED region rule → UNATTRIBUTED) over the frozen OLD probes `verify/baseline/<leg>/probe/old_b*.ps2st` and NEW probes from a copy of the CURRENT Release binary (`build-release/app/host/nativeee` of HEAD 431ef54, md5 `d34dd8411a1fead1162db47f4112a245`; coldboot `new_b120` hash `0x6371becdb140620a` = S6's; the anchors' hashes changed with DP-24, e.g. f46949 `new_b60 0x18ddcd94dffe4d74`). The 24 pairs total **463,533** words = S6's `outside_mask_words` (per-pair totals unchanged: DP-24 changed the pad seq words' VALUES, not the differing set — the 2 words per anchor pair are now kind DP). Kinds: ROW = a §7.1 OLD-defect row; DP = a decided DP; OPEN = an OPEN decision; MIXED = a named region/writer, cause not decided (not attributed per word); UNATTRIBUTED = no rule. **Not attributed = MIXED + UNATTRIBUTED** is a column of its own so the table cannot be read as "99 % attributed".

**Totals: 463,533 words = ROW 7,007 (1.5 %) + DP 44 + OPEN 391,648 (84.5 %) + MIXED 64,228 + UNATTRIBUTED 606 → not attributed 64,834 (14.0 %); attributed (ROW+DP) 7,051 (1.5 %).** The P8.11-B headline (ROW 360,987 = 77.9 %) is withdrawn: 354,000 of those words were relocation classes booked to the SESSION-14 heap row by fiat (review MAJOR 3) and 30,027 of them lay in FREE pool chunks (BLOCKER).

**The coldboot b120 relocation proof, restated (`ledger/reloc_coldboot_b120.txt`; 432,344 RAM0 words outside the mask).** *Heap `0x31afb8`:* 48 of 48 NEW blocks at identical (addr, size) on both sides; OLD's two extra used tail blocks `0xa83ff0` (0x3700) and `0xa87700` (0x4600) — the SESSION-14 hook-induced allocations the §7.1 row records — hold **0 differing words** (both sides all-zero there), so the heap row's recorded words at b120 are its 10 header words (`TITLE-HEAP-HEADER`, ROW) and nothing else: **the ≈ 300 k relocation words are the RECORD POOL's, which the heap row does not record.** *Pool `0x31b138`:* 53 used records at identical (addr, size, idx) — every F:60–62 record (the 40 × 0x2080, the `0x6180`/`0x6080` windows idx 0x29/0x2a, the 8 × 0xa080) and the first F:96 records (idx 0x33/0x34) are layout-identical; **the first layout divergence is used record #53: OLD `0x102cf00`+0x6080 idx 53 = OLD `[ALLOC-TRACE] seq=67 F:96` (the populate burst: after five alloc(0x40)/free pairs at idx 0x35 seq 57–66, the first of four 0x6080 records seq 67–70) | NEW `0x102cf00`+0x600 idx 130.** The review's reading "idx 53–58 = the F:61–62 loader window buffers" is refuted by OLD's own trace: idx 53–56 are F:96 allocations (seq 67–70), idx 0x29/0x2a are the F:61 windows and they match. Classes: `reloc-content` 182,822 (42.3 %) + `reloc-rehandled` 111,838 (25.9 %) = **294,660 (68.2 %) both-sided relocation-equal**; `unmatched-record` 11,403 (2.6 %: the same-idx/other-size pairs idx 55/58/99 — 0x6080 vs 0x2080, i.e. the handle order of the window-sized records differs); `unpaired-record` 10,474 (2.4 %: 10/10 records with no counterpart — OLD idx 54/56 0x6080, NEW idx 54/56 0x6080 at other addresses with other bytes, plus 0x100/0x140/0x1c0 small records); `free-residue` 35,584 (8.2 %: stale bytes in free/pending chunks, 24,664 free on both sides); `genuine` 30,616 (7.1 %: the DP-5 ring tail 5,889 + second ring 17,888 inside matched heap blocks, and 6,822 pointer/value words — the `0x3dc620`+0x2710 block's pointers into the page pool `0xc1c6ec → 0xc166ec`); `other` 49,607 (11.5 %: 48,562 page-pool slab words + statics). **Verdict: the pool layout difference is a relocation of the same content for 68.2 % of the b120 words, with the divergence located in the F:96 populate burst — the same frame as the OLD-only SESSION-14 heap populate — but the pool-side CAUSE (NEW's first differing F:96 pool allocation) is NOT named: NEW's F:96 pool sequence was not obtained (two lldb attempts hung at launch, the I8.4-B class; `int_work/p812b/lldb_pool.lldb` is the ready recipe). Until then every relocation class is OPEN (`OPEN-RELOC-*`), nothing of it is ROW.**

**The writer census (anchors, `census_summary.txt`).** Writers of the formerly UNATTRIBUTED words, by function: `FUN_002057e0` 359 / `FUN_00204df0` 351 / `FUN_00205670` 184 / `FUN_00204dc0` 109 (words `0x31b014-0x31b0f0` + `0xb85a2c-0xb8a4c0` = the page-pool allocator's statics and page descriptors — INT-P811B-SLAB-WALK's class, now `PAGE-POOL-ALLOCATOR-STATICS` / `PAGE-POOL-DESCRIPTORS`, MIXED); `FUN_001e1f48` 81 / `FUN_001e18e8` 55 / `FUN_001e2958` 13 / `FUN_001e7dd0` 2 (`0x2e76e0-0x2e7c48`, `0x3b1fcc`, `0x3b27ec` = the EE sound/STREAM client — INT-P811B-SOUND-STATICS's writer named: `SOUND-CLIENT-STATICS`, MIXED under OPEN-STREAM-CLIENT-STATICS); `FUN_001e5c50` 44 / `FUN_001df020` 39 (`0x3b115c-0x3b1450` = `STREAM-CLIENT-TABLE`); `FUN_001b7088` 25 / `FUN_001b54c0` 20 / `FUN_001b96b8` 12 + libgraph `FUN_002c6700`/`FUN_002c6628` 8+8 (`0x37063c-0x373640` = `RENDER-PACKET-STATICS`); small writers `FUN_001c9048` (`0x3b0420`), `FUN_0026fb40`/`FUN_0018e808` (`0x3bd820`), `FUN_0027ae68` (`0x3bd870`), `FUN_00199938`/`FUN_001999b0` (`0x3097f4`, the T0 ISR family), `FUN_00252080` (`0x309d6c`), `FUN_002c8230` (`0x2e9d98`), `FUN_001e9f90` (`0x3d6bb0`); and **3 words `0x3b0460-0x3b0470` whose writer the census could not resolve to a recompiled function (`WordWatch::Record` as the nearest symbol) = a host-side store (engine/HLE), class `HOST-WRITER`**. Words with NO store in the 61-frame window (134): the sound statics `0x2e7600-0x2e7a00`, the STREAM table `0x3b1100-0x3b1200` (artdet, 44) and page-pool pages — their difference is inherited from the restored image or earlier (`*-INHERITED` classes). Remaining anchor UNATTRIBUTED after the census: **41** = `0x3b03f0-0x3b045c` + `0x3bd840-0x3bd86c` (slowspot_f37092 b2, 32 words: two float 4×4 matrices differing in the low mantissa bits — DP-21's operand-upstream shape; proposal INT-P812B-FLOAT-MATRIX), `0x2e7688-0x2e76a4` (6, sound statics below the rule), `0x31b118`, `0xf589e0/0xf58a00`. Coldboot UNATTRIBUTED **565** (b120 562 + b60 3): `0x31b000-0x31c000` 300, `0x320000-0x321000` 146, `0xf58000` 33, `0x3b0000` 26, `0x370000` 16, `0x3d9000` 16, `0x2e7000` 12 … — no coldboot census was run (one `--frames 121` diag leg with these words is the next command; the list is `ledger/coldboot_b120.unattributed.txt`).

**Verdict per lane/window for the re-baseline rule (DR-1): NO window is 100 % attributed to OLD-defect rows — all 24 pairs BLOCKED.** The blocking classes, by window: *coldboot b60* (214 words): ROW 44 (T0 classes 33, STREAM counters/poll 11) vs MIXED 164 (SPAD stack 89, page-pool slabs 65, descriptors 9) + OPEN 3 (pool header/count) + UNATTRIBUTED 3 — blocking = the slab walk + SPAD stack residue; *coldboot b120* (432,515): ROW 5,980 (ring tail 5,889, heap header 10, T0/STREAM words) vs OPEN 376,846 (the relocation classes 352,117 + second ring 17,888 + genuine 6,822 + pool header 16) + MIXED 49,127 (slabs 48,562, descriptors 378, SPAD 171) + UNATTRIBUTED 562 — blocking = the pool cause (one named allocation would move 294,660 words to a row) and the slab walk; *every anchor pair*: ROW 16–77 + DP 2 vs MIXED 109–3,181 (SPAD data ≈ 70 on every pair = P8.10-A's SPAD work records, page-pool slabs up to 2,435, the writer-named statics) + OPEN up to 5,910 (`GENUINE` = matched bump/pool records whose words no relocation explains: f46949 b60 5,906, slowspot_f37092 b2 4,577, f40391 b60 1,358) — blocking = the per-frame bump arenas' content (the title's frame work, DP-19/DP-21 upstream) and the slab walk.

**Proposals (nothing decided):** INT-P812B-POOL-CAUSE (name NEW's first differing F:96 pool allocation — `lldb_pool.lldb` on a session where lldb launches, or a diag-build pool recorder on `FUN_00202bd8`/`FUN_00202e28` — then a §7.1 row for the pool layout and `@reloc-class` directives booking reloc-content/rehandled/free-residue/unmatched/unpaired to it); INT-P812B-HEAP-ROW-SCOPE (the SESSION-14 row's mem scope "the heap layout … every later heap address" records 10 header words at b120 — reword to the header + the two all-zero tail blocks); INT-P811B-SLAB-WALK (walk the page pool `0xb8c560-0xf58560` by its descriptors `0xb84be0-0xb8c560`, writers `FUN_00204df0/FUN_002057e0/FUN_00205670/FUN_00204dc0` — the largest MIXED class on every pair); INT-P812B-STREAM-CLIENT-STATICS (writers `FUN_001e1f48/FUN_001e18e8/FUN_001e5c50/FUN_001df020`: decide whether these are DP-20's class — the EE client's view of the dead OLD heartbeat — with a per-word record); INT-P812B-HOST-WRITER (which engine/HLE path stores `0x3b0460-0x3b0470`); INT-P812B-FLOAT-MATRIX (the two matrices, DP-21 operand capture); INT-P812B-COLDBOOT-CENSUS (the 565 coldboot words); INT-P812B-PAD-SEQ-ROW (DP-24's proposed `P5-ANCHORS-OLD-PAD-SEQ-RESTART` row: the 2 words per anchor pair become ROW when it lands).

### LEDGER — P8.13-B: the recurring classes attributed STRUCTURALLY (2026-10-04; `int_work/p813b/`; tools `verify/slab_walk.py`, `verify/spad_stack.py`, `attribute_probe.py` `@slabpool`/`@spad-fill`/NONSTATE column)

**What changed.** The same 24 pairs (NEW probes regenerated from `build-release/app/host/nativeee` md5 `03d85242…` of HEAD `bfb0e9f`; f46949 `new_b60` hash `0x18ddcd94dffe4d74` = P8.12-B's, total **463,533** words unchanged) re-attributed with two new structural walks run from BOTH images: the PAGE POOL (`slab_walk.py`: header `0x31aff8`, 972 × 4 KB pages at `0xb8c560`, 32-byte descriptors at `0xb84be0`, u16 class table at `0xf58560`, 63 class lists; the walk asserts the allocator's own invariants — free-page stack == header count `+0x24` (OLD 802 / NEW 809 at cb120), every page on exactly one list, class table == list, per-page free-chunk list == descriptor `+0x14`) and the SCRATCHPAD STACK (`spad_stack.py`: the boundary thread's saved `$sp` from THRD on both images, the crt0 fill `0xa1b2c3d4` at `0x100070-0x100094` bounding the live frames). Every structural class is kind OPEN until a `@reloc-class` directive books it — none is booked; the proposals are below. MIXED fell 64,228 → **1,381** (the MIXED regions `PAGE-POOL-SLABS`/`PAGE-POOL-DESCRIPTORS`/`SPAD-DATA`/`SPAD-STACK` are now structural OPEN classes), UNATTRIBUTED 606 → **124** (coldboot 86 + anchors 38; the 300 `0x31b2xx-0x31b6f4` words are the record pool's handle table — every value a META descriptor pointer `0x200xxxx` on both sides — and the 146 `0x3207ec-0x320a30` words a u16 handle map holding OLD `0x39` where NEW holds `0x35`, i.e. handle + 4 — the four OLD-only F:96 records below). ROW 7,007 → **7,005**: the heap-header rule `0x31afb8-0x31b020` had absorbed the page pool's free-page head/count (`0x31b014`/`0x31b01c`); the rule is now `0x31afb8-0x31aff8`. **(1) SPAD-DATA and SPAD-STACK = DEAD STACK below the live `$sp`, the stack growing down THROUGH THE 16 KB MIRROR — NON-STATE under three per-pair conditions (proved on every anchor pair; CORRECTED by P8.14-B after the P8.13-B review's MAJOR 1/2).** Both images of every anchor pair carry the boundary thread's saved `$sp = 0x70004220` (THRD, tid 1, chain `FUN_001a0150 ← FUN_0019fd58 ← FUN_0017d558 ← FUN_0017ce50`, identical on both sides; the other thread, tid 2, has `$sp` in RAM — NEW `0x3fd0f0/0x3fd100` — or no context); the EE selects the 16 KB scratchpad by `(addr & 0x70000000) == 0x70000000` with the offset in the low 14 bits (NEW `Scratchpad::Offset`, OLD the same), so the live stack pointer is SPAD offset `0x220` and a callee pushing more than `0x220` bytes WRAPS to the top of the scratchpad (`0x70004220 − 0x258 = 0x70003fc8`): a word's distance below `$sp` is `(0x220 − o) & 0x3fff`. The images show it: `0x230` holds `0x19fd6c` (the `ra` `FUN_001a0150` saves at `sp+0x10`), `0x240` `0x17d68c`, `0x260` `0x17d0e4` — the chain's three frames `[0x220, 0x270)` — and `[0x270, 0x1550)` is the crt0 fill `0xa1b2c3d4` on both images (the outermost chain frame `FUN_0017ce50` has NO guest caller frame: the host enters the thread body; the fill run is the span the stack never reached). The differing SPAD words of every anchor pair lie in `[0x8, 0x214)` (the 70/68/74 former `SPAD-DATA`) and in `[0x2ee8, 0x3ff8)` (the 18–321 former `SPAD-STACK`; down to `0x2b20` on f46949 b60 and `0x2b90` on slowspot_f37092 b2): the former are `0xc`–`0x218` below `$sp`, the latter `0x228`–`0x1338` below it through the wrap (`0x1700` / `0x1690` on those two pairs; `dead_evidence.max_distance_below_sp` per pair) — all within the first WRITTEN offset above the fill run, `0x1550` on both images (`0x2cd0` below `$sp` through the mirror — the span ever written since the crt0 fill, stack or the decoder's earlier work buffers, NOT the stack's proven reach; the proven frame depth is the dead words' max wrap distance `0x1338`–`0x1700`; the span `[0x1550, 0x2ee8)` is written but EQUAL on both sides: restored content) [P8.15-B wording, review MINOR 5]; the wrap is visible in the values (NEW `0x3fe0/0x3fe8 = 0xc95734/0xe168a0` = the saved registers at `0x50/0x58`; `0x3ff0 = 0x2a9a10` a return address; `0x3f68` OLD `0x98c140` / NEW `0xbb1560` = `0x0008`) and structurally: both images restored the same SPAD bytes, so a DIFFERING word was written after the restore — boot-path residue cannot differ (the P8.13-B class `spad-dead-above-chain` / `DEAD-STACK-BOOT` was wrong and is withdrawn). Frame shape of the dead words (per pair, `dead_evidence` in the JSON — counted over all differing SPAD words, the 13 masked ones of `0x70003ef0-0x70003f44` included): 23–25 of them hold `.text` addresses (`0x100000-0x2d9a08`; P8.14-B's 28–31 counted the whole PT_LOAD image to `0x30a0b8`, i.e. 5–6 `.rodata`/`.sdata` pointers too — review MINOR 2), 66–117 of 77–229 8-byte slots have a zero high word on both sides (the `sd`-saved 64-bit register shape of EE frames). The live span `[0x220, 0x270)` holds **0 differing words on all 22 anchor pairs**. **The scratchpad is DUAL-USE in this title (its code read in the recomp), so the dead-stack verdict needs conditions, evaluated per pair from the images (`spad_stack.py`, columns `C1 C2 C3`):** (a) `FUN_002ae318` (installed through the pointer table word `0x3068ec`) is a SPAD-stack CONTEXT SWITCH — `memcpy(save+0x1f0, 0x70000000, 0x4000)`, `$sp` saved at `save+0x41f4` and relocated into the copy by `daddu $sp,$v1` (`0x2ae438/0x2ae47c/0x2ae508/0x2ae54c/0x2aeab0/0x2aeaf4`), a call-out under DI/EI, `memcpy` back, `$sp` restored — while it is active the live `$sp` is in RAM: **C1 = the boundary `$sp` is in the scratchpad domain on both images and equal** (holds on all 22 anchor pairs: `0x70004220`; fails on coldboot: NEW carries no thread context — `spad-sp-unproved` 89/171); **C2 = no other thread's `$sp` lies in the scratchpad** (holds everywhere; tid 2 `0x3fd0f0/0x3fd100` or no context); (b) `FUN_002b6fd8` = `_clearOnce` — the MPEG decoder's context init, called by `sceMpegCreate` (`FUN_002b70a8`) at `0x2b7214` — installs the scratchpad as the decoder's work buffers when `ctx+0x87c == 0` (`+0x5a0 = 0x70000000`, `+0x5a4 = 0x70001800`, `+0x6e0 = 0x70001b00`, `+0x6e4 = 0x70003300`, `+0x830 = 0x70003600`; `ctx+0x87c == 1` → RAM buffers at `ctx+0x880/+0xb80/+0xe80` uncached), and the IPU DMA (`_ch3dmaCSC` = `FUN_002b9268`: `0x700003ff → IPU_CMD`, `0x70000000 → ch MADR`) streams through them while a picture decodes — **C3 = no decode in flight: IPU DMA channels 3/4 (fromIPU/toIPU) have CHCR.STR clear in the DMAC section of both images (an image without a DMAC section proves nothing) AND no saved frame (function or return address) of ANY thread's chain — every THRD record with a context, both images — lies in the decoder libraries' text, the WHOLE libmpeg + libipu band of `hle/sdk_bindings.tsv` `0x2b6fd8-0x2c15f8` (libmpeg.a `_clearOnce` … `_pictureDisplayExtension 0x2c0ec0`, libipu.a `setD3_CHCR 0x2c0fb8` … `sceIpuInit 0x2c13c0`, whose body ends at the next function `0x2c15f8`)** (holds on all 24 pairs; P8.15-B — P8.14-B had evaluated the boundary chain only against `0x2b6fd8-0x2bc1f0`, 40 of the 89 libmpeg functions and no libipu function: review MAJOR 1; the counts did not change); the decoder contexts carrying the scratchpad signature are COUNTED, not assumed absent: **two on every anchor pair, on both sides (`0x4a8500`, `0x9b41b0`, flag 0)**, none on coldboot — their presence is not a decode (the buffers are live only while `sceMpegGetPicture` runs; if C3 failed the non-live words would be classed `spad-decoder-work`, guest STATE of the FMV path). The ELF scan of P8.13-B was wrong: the title has non-balanced `$sp` writers — `FUN_002ae318` `daddu $sp,$v1` ×6 (the switch), `FUN_002cab70` `subu $sp,$sp,$v1` (`0x2caba0`, alloca) / `daddu $sp,$fp` (`0x2cad00`), `lui $sp,0; addiu $sp,$sp,4096` at `0x2d6848/0x2d698c` and `lq $sp,464($k0)` at `0x2d6954` (the kernel-side handler thunk / context restore) — none of which is active at a boundary where C1 holds. Totals (P8.14-B, 24 pairs): `spad-dead-below-sp` **3,105** (= the former 1,544 + 1,561; per anchor pair 88–390), `spad-live-frame` **0**, `spad-decoder-work` **0**, `spad-sp-unproved` **260** (coldboot). Why the writers could not be named: the `--watch-word` census observes FastMem's main-RAM fast path only (`fast_mem.inl` `Write<T>`; scratchpad stores take `WriteSlow`) — a 3-frame artdet leg over the 98 differing SPAD words recorded 0 hits; proposal INT-P813B-WATCH-NO-SPAD. Coldboot: NEW's `--state-every` images carry NO thread context (`THRD` tid 1 `has_ctx 0`; OLD's carry `sp 0x70003f60`, chain depth 5 ending in `0x100008`) → proposal INT-P813B-COLDBOOT-THRD-NO-CTX. Finding INT-P813B-ANCHOR-SP-ABOVE-TOP (corrected): the anchors' `$sp 0x70004220` is 0x220 ABOVE the crt0's stack top `0x70004000` on both trees; the title's balanced `addiu sp` frames and the `$sp` writers above cannot produce it from `0x70004000`, and the reference (PCSX2, paused at `0x2ad8e0`) runs with `sp = 0x70003e10` and `0x70004000+` unmapped (reads 0) while its SPAD bottom still holds the fill — the +0x220 is an OLD artefact baked into the anchor states that NEW restores faithfully (the frames of the host-entered chain sit at the SPAD bottom through the mirror; on the console they would sit at the top). Not a score item (both sides equal), recorded.

**(2) PAGE-POOL-SLABS — the slab walk (every pair).** Classes over the 24 pairs: `slab-alloc-differs` **43,440** (coldboot 37,435 + anchors 6,005: free on one side / used on the other, or another class — objects that exist on one side only = the loader's progress, DP-19/DP-17's domain), `slab-genuine` **6,295** (both used, neither side's chunk content has a counterpart: objects both sides have with different content), `slab-reloc-equal` **5,758** (the same object at another chunk, both-sided), `slab-reloc-partial` 1,046, `slab-free-residue` **880** (free page or free-chunk BODY on BOTH sides — stale bytes no list refers to, NON-STATE candidate) and `slab-free-link` **488** (P8.14-B split, review MAJOR 3: the FIRST word of a free chunk on either side, both sides free — the page's free-chunk list link that the next `alloc` of the class pops: allocator STATE, never NONSTATE; cb120 347 of the former 914, of which 259 free-chunk/free-chunk and 88 free-chunk/free-page), descriptors/header/class-table 1,616. Coldboot b120: OLD 170 in-use pages / 7,063 used chunks vs NEW 163 / 6,971; `slab-alloc-differs` 37,423 of the 48,984 pool-range words. Anchors: f46949 b60 1,493 alloc-differs / 619 genuine / 105 reloc-equal / 162 free-residue; f44111 b60 1,076 / 302 / 77 / 83; f46203 b60 824 / 15 / 22 / 34 (its difference is almost entirely allocation state, not content). `slab-genuine` is the only slab class that is content: 2,268 words on the anchors, the per-pair lists in `ledger/<leg>_b<N>.json` (`slab` block) — the GENUINE-record question of P8.13-A applies to them.

**(3) The record-pool divergence cause (coldboot b120), obtained WITHOUT lldb.** A Debug-diag coldboot leg (`build-diag/app/host/nativeee` of 00:37, `--frames 121 --watch-word 0x323278,0x31b184,0x31b188 --watch-record-from 90 --watch-flush-from 90`, `int_work/p813b/pool_watch/watch.log`, 5,708 lines) recorded every store to the pool's live-record counter: **2,107 ops, all in NEW boundary 112 (+1 at 113, +2 at 116), counter 50 → 345**, alloc sites `FUN_00202bd8+0x1cbc` (612) / `+0xf78` (589), free site `FUN_00203230+0xcb4` (906). Aligned op-for-op with OLD's `[ALLOC-TRACE]` from counter 50 (`difflib`, 2,091 OLD ops F:96–97): **exactly two edits.** (a) OLD-only: **four consecutive `0x6080` allocations (OLD seq 67–70, idx 0x35–0x38) between the 5th and the 6th of THIRTEEN (alloc, free) pairs with the same op pattern** (sizes `0x40`–`0x500` on OLD's side: `0x40, 0xc0, 0x40, 0x40, 0x80, 0x40, 0x40, 0x500` after the four; NEW's sizes are unobserved by the counter watch — the identity is of the op pattern, not of the sizes; OLD `AAAA·(AF)×5·AAAA·(AF)×8`, NEW `AAAA·(AF)×13`); (b) NEW-only: 20 ops (12 alloc / 8 free, net +4) at NEW op #192–211 (counter 108 → 112) inside the `(alloc 0x2100, alloc 0x2080, free 0x2100)` populate loop — four more loop iterations. Both end at 345 used. So the first divergence (used record #53 = OLD idx 53 `0x6080`) IS OLD's four `0x6080` records, inserted into a run of 13 identical small alloc/free pairs at F:96 — the frame and the burst of OLD's hook-appended type-registry populate (the heap row's OLD #63 `FUN_00204530` is in the same burst). The caller of the four is NOT in OLD's trace (`ra=0x2053fc` is the pool wrapper for every line) — the attribution to the `0x268440 [SESSION-14]` hook rests on co-location + OLD-only-ness + NEW's loop being the natural one; what would close it: OLD's trace with a backtrace at seq 67 (the `lldb_pool.lldb` recipe on OLD, bounded) — `ROW-candidate`. NEW's four extra populate records are the title's loader progressing further under poll (DP-17/DP-19's class). Consequence for the directives (proposed in the TSV's comment block, not active): `reloc-content` 182,822 + `reloc-rehandled` 111,838 → the SESSION-14 row (scope widened to "the record pool's layout from used record #53 on"); `free-residue` 35,583 → DT-3 NONSTATE; `unmatched-record` 11,403 + `unpaired-record` 10,474 stay OPEN (OLD's four `0x6080` AND NEW's four extra populate records, not separable per word).

**(4) STREAM statics (artdet b60 census, 269 words, `--frames 61`, `int_work/p813b/stream_census/watch.log`).** `0x2e76e0-0x2e7c48` (sound/voice statics): writers `FUN_001e1f48` (3,355 stores, ≈ 55 per boundary on 60 boundaries) / `FUN_001e7dd0` 46 / `FUN_001e18e8` 21 — the EE STREAM reply processor rewrites them every frame; values are voice/handle bytes (`0x6a/0xed/0x132/0x33`; the `0x2e79a8` byte table OLD `0x01010101` vs NEW `0x00000101` on slowspot_f40298: OLD's slots stay marked, NEW's are released) — the EE client's view of the module's replies: **DP-20's class (OLD's dead post-restore heartbeat releases nothing) — ROW-candidate `P5-ANCHORS-OLD-HEARTBEAT-DEAD-AFTER-RESTORE` with the writer named; the per-word record is this census.** `0x3b147c-0x3b17e8` (STREAM-REPLY-BLOCK): writers per FUNCTION (862 stores) `FUN_001e5c50` 621 / `FUN_001df430` 113 / `FUN_001e13b0` 50 / `FUN_001df7a0` 33 / `FUN_001df020` 33 / `FUN_001e6130` 12 (per SITE: `FUN_001e5c50+0x5b4` 619, `+0x968` 2; `FUN_001df7a0+0x236c` 29; `FUN_001df020+0xe84` 20; `FUN_001df430` spread over 8 sites 23/23/23/22/17/2/2/1 — P8.15-B re-tally of `watch.log`, review MINOR 3; 10–48 stores per boundary) — per-slot records of the EE STREAM client (sequence counters 64 apart `0x142/0x102`, lap counters `0x1cd/0x194`, `±1.0f` flags): **DP-8 (5)'s lap-derived counters (the ZERO-POLLS row) — ROW-candidate with the writers named.** `0x3b1100-0x3b147c` (STREAM-CLIENT-TABLE): **0 CPU stores in 61 frames** on artdet (P8.12-B found `FUN_001e5c50/FUN_001df020` on f40391/slowspot_f40298) — on artdet it is written by the SIF RPC reply path, not EE code (the census sees CPU stores only) → the module's reply content: DP-20/DP-8 (5) class, OPEN until the SIF reply copy is instrumented. `0x3b318c-0x3b3200`: 60 distinct store addresses (the 15 words byte by byte), **3,660 stores with NO recompiled function on the host stack (`WordWatch::Record` nearest), 61 per word over 60 boundaries = 4 BYTE stores per word per frame = ONE reply per frame copied byte-wise** → the host-side copy of the STREAM RPC reply into the title's receive buffer; P8.14-B names the writer: `games/<title>/iop/stream_irx/stream_rpc_server.cpp` `StreamRpcServer::Dispatch` — `for i < req.recv_size: write_(req.recv + i, result[i])` with `req.recv/recv_size` = the title's own `sceSifCallRpc` arguments (`engine/sif/hle/sce_sif.cpp` `SceSif::CallRpc`); on retail the IOP sifcmd's reply is SIF1-DMA'd into exactly that buffer — a MODELLED hardware write the title asked for, not a host-only writer and not a NEW defect (the byte granularity vs the DMA's quadwords leaves the same end state); the per-word difference is the module's reply CONTENT: already inside the ROW rule `STREAM-REPLY-BUFFER 0x3b3000-0x3b44a0` (DP-8 (5) recorded) — consistent. 124 of the 269 words had no CPU store in 61 frames (`0x2e771c…`, `0x2e7a0c…`: inherited / DMA-written).

**DT-3 (PROPOSED, the orchestrator decides) — a NON-STATE score column.** Words PROVED not to be guest state are reported in a column of their own in `attribute_probe.py` (kind `NONSTATE`, `nonstate_proved`, printed as `NONSTATE(proved, DT-3 column)=N`), never folded into "attributed (ROW+DP)", never excluded from the total, and a window with any such word stays `BLOCKED` for the re-baseline rule (DR-1) until the column is decided. The proof classes and their evidence (P8.14-B counts): `spad-dead-below-sp` (3,105 words: below the boundary thread's live `$sp` on both images through the 16 KB mirror, issued only where the per-pair conditions C1–C3 of (1) hold — all 22 anchor pairs; the former `spad-dead-above-chain` class is withdrawn, its words are these); `slab-free-residue` (880: free page or free-chunk BODY on both sides by the page pool's own lists — the 488 free-chunk LINK words are `slab-free-link`, allocator STATE, and stay OUT of the column); `free-residue` (35,583: free/pending record-pool chunks on both sides by the pool's own flags/free lists; the record pool keeps its free link in the META descriptor, so its chunk words carry no link). What the column does NOT claim: that these words are harmless to the title — a title that read below its `$sp` or a freed chunk would be a title bug visible on both sides equally; a word that is NONSTATE at the boundary can be state one frame later. Toggle: the three `@reloc-class … DT-3 NONSTATE` directives in `verify/attribution/classes.tsv` (commented out; P8.14-B dropped the `spad-dead-above-chain` line). `verify/score.py` is unchanged (the score keeps counting them); the proposal is to show the column next to the score, not to subtract it.

**Row / fix proposals (nothing decided):** INT-P813B-POOL-CAUSE-ROW (the SESSION-14 row's scope widened to the record pool: OLD's four F:96 `0x6080` records, with the structural proof in (3); the `@reloc-class` lines in the TSV comment; what closes it: OLD's seq-67 backtrace); INT-P813B-DT3 (the NONSTATE column, above); INT-P813B-STREAM-DP20-ROW (the `0x2e76e0-0x2e7c48` voice statics → the DP-20 row with writer `FUN_001e1f48`; the `0x3b147c-0x3b17e8` records → the ZERO-POLLS row with writers `FUN_001e5c50/FUN_001df430/FUN_001e13b0/FUN_001df7a0/FUN_001df020/FUN_001e6130`; both as `recorded:` rules once the rows carry the ranges); INT-P813B-WATCH-NO-SPAD (`engine/core/diag` + `fast_mem.inl`: the write observer should also see scratchpad stores — the SPAD writers of the dead frames stay unnamed until then); INT-P813B-COLDBOOT-THRD-NO-CTX (NEW's `ThreadSection::Serialize` carries a CpuContext only for `restored_` records: a coldboot `--state-every` image has no thread contexts / boundary flag — a savestate gap for the engine owner); INT-P813B-ANCHOR-SP-ABOVE-TOP (recorded; the anchors' `$sp` is 0x220 above the crt0 top on both trees, the console's is below — the OLD-era host-entry artefact in the anchor states); INT-P813B-SLAB-GENUINE (2,268 anchor words of both-sided slab objects with different content — P8.13-A's GENUINE question; lists in the JSONs); INT-P813B-CTEST-REGISTRATION (`tests/verify/CMakeLists.txt` adds `VerifySlabWalkSelftest`/`VerifySpadStackSelftest`; `build/` was not reconfigured by this stage — the sibling's configure picks them up).

**Row proposals (nothing decided; the structural proof text is ready for `EQUIVALENCE_PLAN.md` §7.1 — the orchestrator writes the rows):**

**(a) `P4-CKPT120-OLD-SESSION14-POPULATE-ALLOCS` widened to the record pool's relocation (coldboot b120).** Proof: the record pool (`@pool 0x31b138`, 385 records, used 345 on BOTH sides, OLD free 40 / NEW free 38 + 2 pending) is walked from its own META descriptors on both images; the first layout divergence is used record #53 — OLD idx 53 at `0x102cf00`, size `0x6080` (OLD `[ALLOC-TRACE]` seq 67, F:96), NEW idx 130 at the same address, size `0x600`; OLD's four consecutive `0x6080` records seq 67–70 (idx 0x35–0x38) are the populate burst (§(3): exactly two edits against NEW's counter-watched op sequence, both ending at 345 used). Every word of the pool range that differs between the images is listed by class in `ledger/coldboot_b120.json` (`reloc.words`): `reloc-content` **182,822** (OLD[x] == NEW[x + delta_old(x)] and NEW[x] == OLD[x + delta_new(x)] — the same record bytes at the shifted place, both sides explained), `reloc-rehandled` **111,838** (as above with the record paired by content under another handle index), **298 of the 380 matched records identical under relocation, 281 moved**; `free-residue` 35,584 (free/pending chunks on both sides); `unmatched-record` 11,403 + `unpaired-record` 10,474 (content with no counterpart on the other side: OLD's four `0x6080` records AND NEW's four extra `0x2100/0x2080` populate records, not separable per word), `genuine` 30,616 (record content that differs in place). Directive text (TSV comment, to uncomment when the row exists): `@reloc-class reloc-content POOL-RELOC-CONTENT P4-CKPT120-OLD-SESSION14-POPULATE-ALLOCS ROW` and `@reloc-class reloc-rehandled POOL-RELOC-REHANDLED P4-CKPT120-OLD-SESSION14-POPULATE-ALLOCS ROW`; `unmatched-record` / `unpaired-record` / `genuine` stay OPEN. What the row must say: OLD's hook-appended populate allocates four `0x6080` records at F:96 that retail's loader does not; every record allocated after #53 lands four handles / `0x18200` bytes later on OLD; the words are relocation-equal content, not state differences. What would close the residual doubt: OLD's trace with a backtrace at seq 67 (the caller is not in OLD's trace — `ra=0x2053fc` is the pool wrapper).

**(b) The STREAM statics (artdet b60 census, 269 words, `int_work/p813b/stream_census/watch.log`, re-tallied).** `0x2e76e0-0x2e7c48` (voice statics): writers `FUN_001e1f48+0x81a4` 1,708 + `+0x3bfc` 1,647 = **3,355** stores on 60 boundaries, `FUN_001e7dd0` 46, `FUN_001e18e8` 21 — the EE STREAM reply processor rewrites them every frame from the module's reply (voice/handle bytes; OLD's slots stay marked, NEW's are released) → **DP-20's class, row `P5-ANCHORS-OLD-HEARTBEAT-DEAD-AFTER-RESTORE`**, rule `0x2e76e0-0x2e7c48|FUN_001e1f48|EE-VOICE-STATICS|<row> recorded:P8.13-B-stream-census|ROW|*` once the row carries the range. `0x3b147c-0x3b17e8` (reply block, per-slot records): writers per function `FUN_001e5c50` 621 (site `+0x5b4` 619), `FUN_001df430` 113, `FUN_001e13b0` 50, `FUN_001df7a0` 33 (`+0x236c` 29), `FUN_001df020` 33 (`+0xe84` 20), `FUN_001e6130` 12 (sequence counters 64 apart, lap counters, `±1.0f` flags) → **DP-8 (5)'s lap-derived counters, the `P5-ANCHOR-OLD-ZERO-POLLS` row**, rule with the writer regex `FUN_001(e5c50|df430|e13b0|df7a0|df020|e6130)`. `0x3b1100-0x3b147c` (client table): 0 CPU stores on artdet — written by the SIF RPC reply path → the module's reply content (DP-20 / DP-8 (5) class), OPEN until the reply copy is censused per word (the writer is named below). `0x3b318c-0x3b3200`: the host-side writer is **NEW's modelled SIF RPC reply, `StreamRpcServer::Dispatch` (`games/<title>/iop/stream_irx/stream_rpc_server.cpp`) copying `recv_size` bytes of the module's result buffer to the title's `sceSifCallRpc` receive buffer** (`SceSif::CallRpc` passes `req.recv/recv_size` from the title's a-registers) — 3,660 stores = 15 words × 4 bytes × 61 frames = one byte-wise reply per frame; **on retail the IOP's sifcmd replies by SIF1 DMA into exactly that buffer, so this is modelled hardware the title asked for, NOT a host write into memory retail never touches → not a NEW defect, not a BUG; no row needed beyond the existing `STREAM-REPLY-BUFFER 0x3b3000-0x3b44a0` DP-8 (5) rule** (the difference is the module's reply content). 124 of the 269 words had no CPU store in 61 frames (inherited / DMA-written): they follow the reply-copy attribution.

**(c) The page-pool slab classes (the only slab allocator whose cause is not yet named).** Anchors: the `slab-alloc-differs` words are objects present on ONE side — f46949 b60 **1,506 of 1,513 used on OLD only (539 OLD-only chunks: 160/368/48/32/400/96-byte classes) vs 7 on NEW**; artdet b2 / slowspot_f40298 b60 a handful each side (52/32/4-byte chunks); and the ZERO-POLLS row's `POLL-LIST-NODE` words ARE page-pool chunks (`0xc95664` = class 12, 52-byte chunk, free on one side / used on the other on every anchor pair — the exact ROW rules take them before the slab walk) → on the anchors the slab allocation difference is the scene's object set under OLD's poll/heartbeat defects (DP-19 / DP-20 class: OLD holds objects NEW released); the per-chunk cause needs the allocator's op sequence, not available from two images → **OPEN, proposal: a diag leg `--watch-word 0x31b01c` (free-page count) + the class-list heads `0x31b030-0x31b124` on f46949 (`--frames 61`) names the frame and the caller of every page allocation that differs.** Coldboot b120: `slab-alloc-differs` 37,423 = **23,400 both used with DIFFERENT classes (pages reassigned: the layout shift), 8,624 used on OLD only (795 chunks; 144/112/400/20-byte classes), 5,399 used on NEW only (298 chunks; 400/108/1360/32-byte)** — OLD's extra and NEW's extra coexist (the populate's registry objects vs the loader progressing further), not separable per word without the sequence: **OPEN; the same diag leg on coldboot from frame 90 (as `pool_watch` did for the record pool) aligns the page-pool sequence against the F:96 burst.** `slab-reloc-equal` 5,758 / `-partial` 1,046 (the same object at another chunk) follow whichever cause moved the layout — booked with (c)'s alloc-differs cause once named, never alone. `slab-genuine` 6,295 (both used, different content) → P8.13-A/P8.14-A's GENUINE question (lists in the JSONs).

### LEDGER — P8.15-B: condition C3 corrected (the whole libmpeg+libipu band, every thread, fail-closed) and re-evaluated on the 24 pairs; DT-3 input restated; the AUDIO LEDGER started (2026-10-04; `int_work/p815b/`; tools `verify/spad_stack.py`, `verify/attribute_probe.py`, NEW `verify/attribute_audio.py`, ctest `VerifyAttributeAudioSelftest`)

**Mem lane — C3 as the tool now evaluates it (P8.14-B review MAJOR 1, MINORs 2/4/5).** `@spad-decoder-text 0x2b6fd8-0x2c15f8` = the whole libmpeg.a + libipu.a band of `games/<title>/hle/sdk_bindings.tsv` (89 libmpeg rows `_clearOnce 0x2b6fd8` … `_pictureDisplayExtension 0x2c0ec0`; 5 libipu rows `setD3_CHCR 0x2c0fb8`, `sceIpuStopDMA 0x2c10a8`, `sceIpuRestartDMA 0x2c1190`, `sceIpuSync 0x2c12e0`, `sceIpuInit 0x2c13c0`; the band ends at the next function `0x2c15f8`, an FPU routine (`swc1`/`mov.s`), not libipu — the next bound SDK function is libgraph `sceGsResetGraph 0x2c1bd8`). `spad_stack.compare` tests EVERY THRD record with a context on both images (function AND return address of every saved frame) and names the thread of a hit; `ipu_dma_busy` returns `None` for an image without a DMAC section and C3 is then NOT EVALUABLE → every SPAD word `spad-sp-unproved` (fail closed; the same without a band). The decode is synchronous on the calling thread (`sceMpegGetPicture 0x2b7c10 → _getpic 0x2b73a8 → _sceMpegPictureData0 0x2bd378 → _slice0 0x2bd5b8`, the IPU DMA kicked from inside), so a decode interrupted at a boundary leaves that thread's chain in the band or the IPU DMA running — the two tests. **Neither tree's image carries an IPU section** (sections of every probe image: `RAM0 SPAD META VRAM VU0C VU0D VU1C VU1D THRD SEMA EVFL INTC DMAC TIMR GSPR GSST VIFS GIFS PADS FILE CNTR HEAP STRM SIFS VU1R SDRX SNDC MCHL IOPM MISC` — no IPU CTRL busy bit on either side; the reviewer's "IPU busy flag in the image's IPU section" does not exist), and libmpeg's inner context carries no "decoding" flag — `_sceMpegPictureData0` resets `+0x820`/`+0x824` at entry and `_slice0` XORs `+0x820` per macroblock row (the scratchpad work-buffer bank toggle), `_getpic` writes `+0x0` (status) and `+0x834`, the slice loop `+0x848/+0x84c` — so the context's words are REPORTED as corroboration (`decoder_ctx_state_*`), not tested. Re-run on the 24 pairs (NEW probes regenerated from a copy of `build-release/app/host/nativeee` md5 `06fcdf48` of 08:05: `probes.log` hashes identical to P8.14-B's, e.g. artdet `new_b60 0x2444ea61231c9af1`, coldboot `new_b120 0x6371becdb140620a`; the ledger totals `| **all 24** | | 463533 | 7005 | 44 | 454979 | 1381 | 124 | 1505 |` and all 24 UNATTRIBUTED lists byte-identical): **all 22 anchor pairs `C1 Y C2 Y C3 Y` with `thr 2/2; band hits none; dma []/[]; ctx 2/2 eq Y`** (both threads of both images tested — OLD tid 1 `0x1a0150/0x19fd58/0x17d558/0x17ce50`, OLD tid 2 no context, NEW tid 1 the same chain, NEW tid 2 `0x1e7f80/ra 0x1e7fdc` — none in the band; the two decoder contexts `0x4a8500`/`0x9b41b0` read `mode=0x0 status=0x1|0x0 bank=0x0 w824=0x0 w834=0x0 dma_src0=0x0 dma_src1=0x0` identically on both sides); coldboot `N Y Y (thr 2/2; … ctx 0/0)`. Counts unchanged: `spad-dead-below-sp` 3,105 / live 0 / decoder-work 0 / unproved 260. `@spad-text` is now the ELF's `.text` `0x100000-0x2d9a08`: 23–25 code-address words per anchor pair (not 28–31). **DT-3 input (final from this stage): NONSTATE candidates `spad-dead-below-sp` 3,105 (22 pairs, C1–C3 proved as above) + `slab-free-residue` 880 (free BODY words; the 488 `slab-free-link` words are allocator STATE, excluded) + `free-residue` 35,583 (record pool; the free link lives in META); excluded as STATE or unproved: 488 free links, 260 coldboot SPAD words (no thread context in NEW's coldboot image).** The orchestrator decides DT-3 on this; nothing booked (the three `@reloc-class … NONSTATE` lines stay comments).

### LEDGER — P8.18-B: the ledger/re-baseline tools corrected per the P8.17-B review (slot-order permutations are STATE; the proof bound to the artefacts; image-only windows; the page-init site); slabs attempt 3; audio content attempt 2; frames Class B attempt 2; the FINAL per-window tables (2026-10-04; `int_work/p818b/`; tools `verify/attribute_audio.py`, `verify/apply_rebaseline.py`, `verify/rebaseline.py`, `verify/slab_census.py`, `verify/attribute_probe.py`, `verify/score.py`, `tools/frame_diff.py`)

**(1) The four MAJORs, fixed and selftested.** *MAJOR 1 — `realloc-permutation` is hash-relevant state:* the class leaves INAUDIBLE for its own category ORDER (`attribute_audio.py`): the lane hashes the raw float PCM (`engine/core/verify/audio_hasher.h`) and the mixer sums the voices in slot order in float (`engine/sound/spu_core.cpp` MixInto), so the same sounds on other slots change the window hash while being perceptually equivalent; the gate block carries `ORDER n` (never NONSTATE), `apply_rebaseline.py` refuses it like OPEN, and a window with no audible voice but permutations reads `OPEN order (permutation N: the same sounds on other slots vXX … the hash-relevant candidate cause observed AT the pair)` — the unobserved UNATTRIBUTED item is added only when there is neither an audible voice nor a permutation; a heartbeat ROW voice plus a permutation is `OPEN order+heartbeat`, never `candidate` (30 selftest checks). *MAJOR 2 — the proof bound to the artefacts:* `attribute_probe.py` records `old_md5`/`new_md5` next to the vsync frames, `attribute_audio.py` the window's `old_window_hash`/`new_window_hash`; `apply_rebaseline.py` refuses unless the frozen probe's md5 + vsync frame equal the ledger's `old_*`, the `--image`'s equal the ledger's `new_*`, the image's chained state hash equals the NEW lane line's (the image is from the recorded leg), the audio lines' hashes equal the ledger's; a ledger without digests, a non-image, an unrelated image, a wrong digest or vsync frame each refuse (selftest 45 checks). On the real layout the reviewer's scenario now refuses: `REFUSED (coldboot mem b120): the image …/probes/artdet_f37152/new_b2.ps2st (md5 73644350b6f1ef854afea4450d1942eb) is not the image the ledger attributed (new_md5 e8e7c4963dcf1b2d547d8b510ff27838)`, while the right image with the probe-set layout is accepted in a dry run: `DRY RUN apply_rebaseline: coldboot mem b120 -> effective reference '120,0x6371becdb140620a' (frozen '120,0x74bacf01b589b5a6' kept) … bound: old_md5 ca950ec0 new_md5 e8e7c496 vf 121/120; build_id PS2RECOMP-BUILD-ID 2026-10-04 11:56:36` (a synthetic 100 %-attributed copy of the real ledger; the real one refuses on its kinds). *MAJOR 3 — image-only windows:* `rebaseline.py` accepts a mem row with `old_line` = `new_line` = `-` and an image (`image_only`): `effective_lines` leaves the lane untouched, `effective_probe` serves the image; `apply_rebaseline.py` writes that shape when neither side has a lane line at the boundary and the frozen probe exists (the anchors' b2); `score.py` scores the pair against the image and reports it (`rebased_pairs=2: artdet_f37152@b2, coldboot@b60` in the selftest). The real artdet b2 pair now reaches the gate: synthetic 100 % ledger → `DRY RUN apply_rebaseline: artdet_f37152 mem b2 -> effective reference '-' (frozen '-' kept) … image-only window (no mem.mhash line at boundary 2 on either side; the image is the reference); bound: old_md5 7396ae96 new_md5 73644350 vf 37159/2`; the real ledger → `REFUSED (artdet_f37152 mem b2): the window is not 100 % attributed: MIXED 1, OPEN 117 (ROW 42, DP 2, NONSTATE 0)` (was: "has no mem.mhash line for key 2"). *MAJOR 4 — the page-init site:* `slab_census.py` takes the real site `FUN_00204d48\+0xa40` (the pool INIT routine building a page's free-chunk list; its store reads `0x0 -> first chunk` on a fresh page = `init_from_zero`), names per reassigned page the init event AND the TRIGGER (the first pop on the page at/after the init, with its size class), reports every UNMATCHED descriptor site (`unmatched_sites`, never silently `other`), records `--census-from` in the JSON and the unobserved classes' notes (MINOR 7), and splits `slab-old-held-new-freed` by the chunk body into `same-object` / `address-only` (MINOR 8); selftest 17 checks (an init store from `0x0`, an unmatched site reported, the trigger, both body outcomes). MINORs 5 (a ledger without `leg` refuses), 6 (`--source` accepts the probe set's `f<N>_<lane>` files; the HANDOFF command below works), 10 (`git describe --always --dirty` + `build_id`) are in; MINOR 9 is answered by (2) and the FINAL tables (the ledger's `SLAB-ALLOC-DIFFERS` is split per census pair in the tables, not inside `attribute_probe`). The 24-pair ledger re-run (NEW probes regenerated from `build-release` md5 `10a8d66a`, hashes identical to P8.17-B's: `PROBE HASHES IDENTICAL to P8.17-B`; with the P8.12-B writer census) is byte-identical in kinds to P8.17-B's: `| **all 24** | | 463533 | 7005 | 44 | 454979 | 1381 | 124 | 1505 |`, VERDICT_TABLE identical; every ledger now carries the digests.

**(2) Slabs attempt 3 — the page-init/reassignment classes attributed (`int_work/p818b/slab_census/`, `page_classes.md`).** Coldboot b120 with the real site: `page inits: 80 on 76 pages (75 from 0x0 = a page's first list); census recorded from b90`, `busiest boundaries: b112 {'alloc': 7385, 'free': 2256, 'page-init': 78}, b113 {'alloc': 80, 'page-init': 1, 'free': 59}, b116 {… 'page-init': 1}`; **every one of the 1,753 `slab-page-reassigned` chunks (23,400 words) now carries its event**: `slab-page-reassigned@b112=22972, @b113=410, @b116=18`, 22,422 words on pages initialised FROM `0x0` (a page that held no list before: taken from the free-page list and threaded for the new class) — the trigger is in every case the first POP of the new class on that page in the same boundary (`b112:FUN_00204dc0+0x328:size144=3978, size112=2859, size36=2023, size20=1869, size400=1825, size2048=1799, size1360=1620, size16=1256, size60=1223, size48=1214, size4096=634, size816=534`; e.g. page 159 `init b112 from 0x0 -> trigger pop 0xc2b560 size 4096`, page 161 `init b113 -> pop 0xc2d560 size 2048`). So the b112/b113 locus is EVIDENCED: NEW's populate burst frees pages (2,256 pushes) and re-threads 76 of them for other size classes, and the requester of every re-threaded page is the first allocation of that class in the burst (the requester's caller is still not recorded — `WordWatch::Site` names the allocator only). *The pages classed at page granularity* (OLD's and NEW's used chunks on each page looked up BY CONTENT among all used chunks of the other image, `page_classes.py`): **both-sided-reordered 11 pages / 4,288 words** (every object on both sides has a content twin on another page of the other image: the same allocations landed on other pages = the layout shift, SESSION-14's consequence), **SESSION-14-candidate 11 / 4,620** (OLD's objects on the page have no twin in NEW: OLD-only allocations of the populate burst — the hook-appended registry's objects; e.g. page 153 `OLD cls 35/144B used 28, twin 0, OLD-only 28`), **NEW-progress 8 / 4,137** (NEW-only objects: the loader further under poll, DP-17/DP-19), **mixed 21 / 10,355** (both kinds on one page: the 16/20/36/48-byte small-object pages where the two bursts interleave). The anchors: 0 page inits in 61 frames (the b0 release burst re-threads no page); `slab-old-held-new-freed` f46949 1,089 = `@b0 790` with the body split `same-object 266 / address-only 807` (f44111 552 = `@b0 446`, `same-object 142 / address-only 401`): most chunks NEW freed at b0 were re-allocated and freed again by NEW before b60 (address-only), so the b0 release's identity with OLD's object is proved for 266 / 142 words only; coldboot's 1,214 are all `address-only` (b90–120 reuse). *Residual slab-genuine* (coldboot 4,016: 2,096 words in 1360-byte chunks of pages 70/72 with no NEW event in b90–120, inherited content; the anchors 632 / 310 likewise mostly no event): OPEN as content (DP-19/DP-21 upstream), unchanged. What this attempt did NOT reach (OPEN, named): OLD's page-pool allocations of F:96 (OLD's trace covers the record pool only; OLD is frozen) and the requester caller of the trigger pops (engine diag: a `Site()` depth-2 column). Three attempts made (P8.13-B structure, P8.17-B census, P8.18-B page-init + page classes): the class is recorded OPEN at the per-page level with the proposal that the SESSION-14 row's scope widen to "the page pool's layout from the populate burst on" ONLY for the both-sided-reordered + SESSION-14-candidate pages (8,908 words; the NEW-progress and mixed pages are NEW-progress/DP-19 words, not OLD-defect words).

**(3) Audio content attempt 2.** *The pitch words (f57292 W0 b1 v31 `0x759` vs `0x8b9`; f46203 W9 b59 OLD v33 `0x759` vs NEW v29 `0x881`):* both windows key the SAME sample `ssa 0x7c800`, and on every instance read OLD keys it at pitch **`0x759`** (f57292 OLD b1 v31; f46203 OLD b59 v33; the anchors' own earlier instance of the sample sits parked at `0x759` on both trees — f46203 anchor v33 Off `ssa 0x7c800 pitch 0x759`, f57292 anchor v31 Off `ssa 0xf7790 pitch 0x759`), while NEW keys it at a DIFFERENT pitch each time (`0x8b9` = ×1.187, `0x881` = ×1.157 of `0x759`); on f57292 NEW's v31 is already in Attack with `0x8b9` at b0 (NEW's first frame after the restore) where the anchor had the slot Off, OLD's b1 image shows the same key-on at `0x759`. Reading: the title's SFX request carries a per-instance pitch (base × a variation); OLD's variation input evaluates to the base every time, NEW's varies — the pitch argument's EE-side producer is the arbiter: a `--watch-word` on the EE sound-request record's pitch field (the sound client `FUN_001e1f48`/`FUN_001df020` family that writes `SOUND-CLIENT-STATICS`) on NEW, and OLD's side from its `[SND]`-class log if one names the request (not read this stage) — candidate domains: DP-19 (a zero poll/lap input feeding the variation), DP-21 (a float path), or OLD's RNG seed; OPEN, class `AUDIO-PITCH-VARIATION` (2 windows). *The 6 former `OPEN-unobserved` windows:* 3 are now `OPEN order` (artdet W1 b9: permutation 3 `v29,v32,v37`; f40391 W8 b48: 4 `v24,v25,v26,v27`; f46203 W9 b59 carries 5 permutations next to its content pair) — the slot assignment of the sound module differs at the pair, the hash-relevant candidate cause; 4 stay `OPEN-unobserved` (f44111 W1, f46949 W1, f59991 W6, slowspot_f37092 W5: bookkeeping / confirmed save-instant KONs only). *The first differing MIXER FRAME:* neither tree has a PCM dump — NEW's `app/host/ee_bootstrap.cpp` has `--dump-frames` but no audio dump knob and no `audio.dump` Config key (`engine/core/config/config.cpp`: `audio.enabled/volume/sdr_block_ms` only); OLD's `host_app/verify/audio_hasher.cpp` hashes only and no `PS2_*DUMP` env touches audio (`PS2_SPIKE_DUMP/BAKE_DUMP/MPEG_DUMP_N/…` are GS/MPEG) — so OLD's side cannot be dumped from the frozen tree at all, and the per-boundary 800-frame sub-hash cannot be re-derived from images. Proposal (engine, not owned): a `--dump-audio <dir>` harness knob writing the hashed float frames per boundary on NEW (`f<boundary>.pcm`), paired with a slot-order-insensitive compare (sum the voices in a canonical order under a diag toggle) that would confirm or refute the ORDER class without OLD's PCM: if NEW's hash under canonical order equals the baseline's window hash, the permutation IS the whole difference. OPEN.

**(4) Frames Class B attempt 2 (`tools/frame_diff.py --quantize-a 5 --draws`; `int_work/p818b/frames_*_q5.{txt,json}`).** With OLD's display truncated to 5 bits (the proposed row applied): artdet 37157 residual 163,642 px (100 % multiples of 8), **SUB-STEP (≤ 2 steps) 135,234 px = 82.6 %, OBJECT-level (> 2 steps) 28,408 px** spread over all 56 differing 64-px cells, densest at (192,64) 2,665 px / max 136 and (128,64) 1,647 / 112 (Crash), (320,320) 1,483 / 120 and (320,384) 1,381 (the plant); f46949 46950: 162,859 px, sub-step 127,886 = 78.5 %, object-level 34,973, densest (256,64) 2,462 / 160, (256,128) 2,323 / 144, (192,64) 1,983 / 208. The draws over the object cells (OLD's drawlist of the scene buffer `fbp 0x70000` of the frame the display copy reads, `drawlist_tool dump --frame 0`): the (192,64) cell is covered by 15 draws — `id=66a8cd1e tristrip+tex` ×7 (the world/skinned T8-CLUT textured strips, `mmag=1` bilinear), `id=32a9196d tristrip+tex+abe` ×5 (small alpha-blended textured strips, bboxes 115..215 × 93..169 = Crash's parts), the full-screen `raw sprite` clear and two `raw sprite+tex+abe` passes; the magnitude histogram is a continuous tail (24: 11,421 … 128: 107, max 216) with no cell majority-object except Crash's (65 % of (192,64)). Class: **OPEN** — the sub-step residue is the two renderers' per-pixel rounding under bilinear texture filtering + alpha blending (the GS corpus proves the renderers hash-identical on OLD's batched streams, so the difference is live-stream granularity), and the object-level cells are the same rounding compounded through the alpha-blended Crash/plant passes (`tristrip+tex+abe`), not a missing/moved object (every class's vertex count is identical, P8.15-A); the op is not isolable from the display frames — the next measurement is the per-draw render of the CT32 target `0x70000` at 8 bits on both sides (OLD `PS2_SNAP_RT=70000`, NEW an RT dump knob — engine) diffed draw by draw. No `P2-*` row, no NEW gap found, no OLD gap named: OPEN with the ops named (`FRAMES-SUBSTEP-TEXFILTER-ABE`).

### LEDGER — P8.19-A (the closing stage): the proposed §7.1 rows FILED with their ledger proofs; DT-3 and DT-4 MADE; the proved classes BOOKED in the ledger directives and the 24-pair ledger re-run; every re-baseline dry run REFUSED; gate S8 (2026-10-04; `int_work/p819a/`; data `verify/attribution/classes.tsv`, docs `EQUIVALENCE_PLAN.md` §7.1, `INTEGRATION_READINESS.md`, `INTEGRATION_STATUS.md`, `INTEGRATION_PLAN.md` §6; no tool or engine edit)
