**(2) DT-3 — MADE (P8.19-A, 2026-10-04 14:55).** *Decision:* NONSTATE — words PROVED not to be guest state — is reported as its OWN column of the ledger and of every gate summary, never excluded from the total and never counted as attributed; the proof classes are exactly the three the ledger stages evidenced: `spad-dead-below-sp` (dead stack below every thread's live `$sp` through the 16 KB mirror, issued only where the per-pair conditions C1–C3 hold — all 22 anchor pairs `Y Y Y`; coldboot's 260 `spad-sp-unproved` words are NOT in the column), `slab-free-residue` (free page / free-chunk BODY on both sides; the 488 `slab-free-link` words are allocator STATE and stay OPEN), `free-residue` (free/pending record-pool chunks on both sides; the link lives in META). The score tuple KEEPS `outside_mask_words` as defined (DT-1) — NONSTATE words stay inside it — and the two extra columns `nonstate_words` and `row_attributed_words` are reported next to it from the ledger (this stage: 39,568 and 301,937 over the 24 pairs; `score.py --compare` orders by the original tuple, so no KEEP/REVERT rule changes); the policy's "every word names writer + row" is what the ledger's ROW kind means, and a NONSTATE word is neither — a window with NONSTATE words stays BLOCKED for the re-baseline gate until the OTHER kinds read 0 OPEN / 0 MIXED / 0 UNATTRIBUTED (the gate already behaves so: `apply_rebaseline.py` counts NONSTATE in its parenthesis and refuses on the others). NOT in the column: the audio lane's slot permutations (`ORDER`, hash-relevant STATE — the P8.17-B review's MAJOR 1), free links, unproved SPAD words. *Rationale:* the three classes are proved from the allocators' and the stack's OWN structures on both images, so they are not evidence of a NEW/OLD state difference — but the policy forbids masking guest state, and whether a title ever reads a freed chunk or below its `$sp` is not provable from two images; reporting rather than subtracting keeps the score honest and the proof visible. *Toggle:* the three `@reloc-class … DT-3 NONSTATE` directives in `verify/attribution/classes.tsv` (uncommented by this stage, each with its proof comment). *Measured outcome:* the ledger over the 24 pairs reads NONSTATE **39,568** (`POOL-FREE-RESIDUE` 35,583 + `DEAD-STACK` 3,105 + `SLAB-FREE-RESIDUE` 880) — the P8.14-B/P8.15-B candidate counts exactly; `score.py` is UNCHANGED (its columns are the gate summary's job; emitting them from `score.py` itself is a `verify/` tool edit outside this stage's paths — finding INT-P819A-SCORE-COLUMNS). **KEPT (a reporting decision; no lane moves).**

**(3) DT-4 — MADE (P8.19-A, 2026-10-04 14:55).** *Decision:* the effective-reference mechanism AS BUILT by P8.17-B and corrected by P8.18-B is ADOPTED as the only way a frozen window may ever be re-based: the frozen OLD baseline files are never edited; `verify/baseline/<leg>/REBASELINE.tsv` names each re-based window (lane, window, row ids, NEW commit `git describe --always --dirty`, proof ledger, frozen + NEW lines, NEW probe image under `rebased/`; image-only mem windows with `-` lines); `diff_legs`/`score`/`gate_run`/`battery` compare against the effective reference and REPORT every re-based window; `apply_rebaseline.py` is the ONLY writer and writes a row only when the window's ledger reads 0 OPEN / 0 ORDER / 0 MIXED / 0 UNATTRIBUTED with ≥ 1 ROW/DP word, every booked row is filed in §7.1, the ledger names the leg, and the proof is BOUND to the artefacts (the ledger's `old_md5`/`new_md5` + vsync frames equal the frozen probe's and the installed image's; the image's state hash equals the NEW lane line's; the audio lines' hashes equal the ledger's). *Rationale:* DR-1's rule ("a window is re-baselined from NEW only with a row naming the OLD defect and only after every residual word is attributed") needs a mechanism that cannot be satisfied by assertion; this one refuses by construction unless the ledger proves the window and the artefacts are the ones the ledger attributed. *Toggle:* none needed — the mechanism is inert until a row is written; no `REBASELINE.tsv` exists. *Measured outcome:* dry runs on every window of this stage's ledger (`int_work/p819a/dry_runs.log`, 35 runs, every one `rc=3`): the 24 mem pairs REFUSE — e.g. `REFUSED (coldboot mem b120): the window is not 100 % attributed: OPEN 95643, UNATTRIBUTED 84 (ROW 300638, DP 0, NONSTATE 36150)`, `REFUSED (artdet_f37152 mem b2): … MIXED 1, OPEN 15 (ROW 44, DP 0, NONSTATE 102)`, `REFUSED (ps2state_slot0_f36935 mem b60): … MIXED 62, OPEN 13 (ROW 61, DP 0, NONSTATE 92)` — and the 11 audio windows REFUSE on P8.18-B's unchanged audio ledgers (`REFUSED (artdet_f37152 audio W1): … ORDER 3 (ROW 0, DP 0, NONSTATE 6)`, `… (ps2state_slot0_f57292 audio W0): … OPEN 1`, `… (ps2state_slot0_f44111 audio W1): … UNATTRIBUTED 1`); `git status --short verify/baseline` empty. **No window qualifies today — expected; nothing applied. KEPT.**

**(4) The booking (`verify/attribution/classes.tsv`; nothing by fiat — every directive/rule carries its proof in the comment above it):** `@reloc-class reloc-content POOL-RELOC-CONTENT P4-CKPT120-OLD-SESSION14-POPULATE-ALLOCS ROW` and `@reloc-class reloc-rehandled POOL-RELOC-REHANDLED … ROW` (proof: the coldboot b120 ledger + the pool watch, (1) above); the three DT-3 directives; the two `PAD-SEQ` words → `P5-ANCHORS-OLD-PAD-SEQ-RESTART ROW` (were DP); two WRITER-GATED ROW rules — `0x2e76e0-0x2e7c48 | FUN_001e(1f48|18e8|2958|7dd0) | EE-VOICE-STATICS → P5-ANCHORS-OLD-HEARTBEAT-DEAD-AFTER-RESTORE recorded:P8.13-B(4)-stream-census` and `0x3b147c-0x3b17e8 | FUN_001(e5c50|df430|e13b0|df7a0|df020|e6130) | STREAM-SLOT-RECORDS → P5-ANCHOR-OLD-ZERO-POLLS recorded:P8.13-B(4)-stream-census` (tab-separated: the regex carries `|`) — a word books ONLY where a `--watch-word` census names one of those writers for it; the census dir `int_work/p819a/census/` = P8.12-B's per-pair logs with P8.13-B's artdet-b60 stream census appended; un-censused words of those ranges stay MIXED (`EE-VOICE-TABLE`, `STREAM-REPLY-BLOCK`, `*-INHERITED`). wall-pumped load is an OLD defect by the clock model), so at b1500 OLD is inside the cutscene and NEW at the title. **DT-6 (MADE):** probe pairs and lane windows of a leg whose timelines
differ by a ROWED OLD timing defect are paired by the shared guest clock (`0x323534`, the title's tick word, +149/frame on both trees) — the NEW boundary whose clock word equals the OLD

**DT-6 status (P8.25-T, 2026-10-05): IMPLEMENTED — with a corrected key, and the S9 number stands.** *HW / title note:* the
word at `0x323534` is the title's tick accumulator: it advances **exactly +149 per EE boundary** on BOTH trees while the title
runs (+2048 periodically every ≈ 500 boundaries, +4 across the attract movie + reload; NEW census b1140→b1200 = 60 × 149; OLD's
frozen probes b1500→b2700 = 1200 × 149 + 2 × 2048), but through the boot FMVs and the Beach load each tree pumps it at its own
pace (NEW +1136..+1828 per FMV boundary; OLD ≈ 540 per vsync frame through its wall-pumped boot — the DP-17 class itself), so
at the same scene moment the VALUES differ by ≈ 640 k (OLD's title entry 999,855 at b850; NEW's 360,847 at b1133): **an equal
clock VALUE never occurs inside the leg** (`clock_pair.py --pair`: `exact-clock match: none (NEW census range 0..1097574)` × 5).
The key the text allowed for instead: **the scene EDGE (boot-SM word `0x98c140` case 6 → 7, the Beach load's completion) plus
the clock's advance since it** — the title's scene timers count ticks from that edge (the attract idle fires 2939 boundaries
after it on both trees). Edges imaged to the boundary on both sides: OLD b849 case 6 (`0x001c614000010000`) / **b850** case 7
(`0x0060718000010000`, vf 1380) = NEW b1132 / **b1133** (the same two words); pass 2 OLD **b3833** / NEW b4115 case 6 / **b4116**
case 7 → **offset +283 on both edges** (the inter-edge distance 2983 is identical: title idle + 43 movie boundaries + the
reload). Landed: `verify/clock_pair.py` (census / exact match / edge pairing / scene check / the 149-law residual; `--keys`
reads the two guest addresses from the leg's `SCENE_KEYS.tsv` — title data, LintEnginePurity-clean), `LEG.tsv` optional

old_b5400=new_b5683`) + `vf_offset` (530) columns (`leg_spec.py`), `probe_images.py` (NEW image at the mapped boundary,

every pair), `diff_legs.py` (lanes paired from the edges by the offset; `…, scene-paired: offset +283 from old_b850=new_b1133`
on the verdict line; mem lines UNPAIRED past the edge because 283 is off the 60 cadence — the probe pairs carry the lane).

own ≈ 15-frame uncertainty on A) — OPEN, next stage. **Loop 1 (P8.25-T review, 2026-10-05): the pins LANDED** as the ctest

*Evidence:* the ring-cache gate (P8.29-A, `loader.ring_cache=on`) moved NEW's boot timeline (fewer drive requests: `loader_reads
5/170` vs 5/906 on coldboot; `audio_first_nonsilent_window` 191 → 177; the title footprint jump b1080 instead of b1140) while

PAIRING.tsv`). *Options:* (a) derive the edge per run from an image census `--frames <frozen NEW edge + margin> --state-every 1`
read image by image and pruned as it lands (chosen: exact to the boundary, deterministic under the profile, no engine change;
~0.25 s/boundary of image I/O ≈ 5 min); (b) a `run.meta` census field `boot_sm_case7_b=` emitted by the harness when the SM
word first reads 7 (the zero-cost exact method — an app/host edit, SPECIFIED for a builder stage: `app/host/ee_bootstrap.cpp`
boundary hook, the word `(u64 @ SCENE_KEYS.boot_sm_word >> 44) & 0x3f`, title data from `games/`; once present
`scene_pairing.derive` reads it instead of running the census); (c) the lanes (rejected: frame hashes are not portable
between runs — the load screen animates and the title frames differ by render class; the audio onset lags the edge by 1–2
windows; mem every 60); (d) bisection with one-image legs (rejected: 7+ full boots ≈ 8 min, no cheaper). *Rule:* the edge is the first case-7 image whose
DENSE predecessor (b − 1 PRESENT in the census) reads case 6 — a harness-refused or unreadable image at the edge gives no edge, never
one a boundary late; the missing boundaries are named on the census log line (`census_gaps`; both real censuses were dense, 1283/1283).

## DT-7 — The KEEP/REVERT decision compares UNBOOKED outside-mask words: the raw count MINUS the words booked to FILED §7.1 rows whose OLD-side cause is CONSOLE-PROVED, word by word; the raw count stays the gate's reported number (2026-10-06, P8.30-T)

**Evidence.** P8.29-A's ring-cache model is console-exact on its class (`words differing CON vs NEWON: 4 / 31870` over the
Default.rm2 ring; the loader object's words equal the console's) yet the gate scored it WORSE vs OLD (`coldboot b120 +2,326`:
the class vs OLD rose 17,888 → 19,472 because OLD mirrors every served read at the ring base — `hle_heap.cpp:1442-1470`, OLD ≠
console too). Under the raw rule every console-correct change that moves NEW AWAY from an OLD defect scores worse, so the rule
would REVERT the console. The user's standing rule: PCSX2 is the reference, OLD is evidence.

**Decision (MADE).** `score.py` reports per pair `words_raw` (= `total`, the DT-1 count, UNCHANGED), `words_booked_console` and
`words_unbooked = raw − booked_console`, and prints TWO tuples: `tuple = (legs_identical, outside_mask_words, pins)` — the
REPORTED number, Section 6's definition unchanged — and `tuple_unbooked = (legs_identical, outside_mask_words_unbooked, pins)`,
the number `score.py --compare` DECIDES on (both lines printed; a score before DT-7 decides on the raw tuple and says so). A word
is `booked_console` when (1) `attribute_probe.py` books it (first-matching-rule order, the ledger's) to a ROW rule / `@reloc-class`
directive whose §7.1 row is FILED and carries `proof=console:<raw EE image>@<leg>:b<N>` — the PCSX2 image matched to THAT probe
pair (the FILED condition is ENFORCED by `attribute_probe.load_rules`: a proof token on a row id `diff_legs.known_row_ids()` does
not resolve is REFUSED, as `apply_rebaseline.py` refuses an unfiled row; review loop 1) — AND (2) the console image's word at the address equals NEW's and differs from OLD's (`attribute_probe.console_booking`,
checked per word: a row's proof is never asserted onto a word the console does not confirm; SPAD words are not provable from a
RAM dump; another pair's proof never applies). The booking is computed in `gate_run.py` while the pair's images exist
(`attribute_probe.booked_console_only`: 0 at once for a pair without a proved rule) and carried in `probe_words.json`.

**What it is NOT.** Not a mask (every word stays in `outside_mask_words`; `verify/mask/` untouched; the DP-15 rule stands). Not a
re-baseline (no frozen file or `REBASELINE.tsv` row; DT-4's 100 % rule for re-basing a window stands — a booked word is still a
divergence from OLD). Not Section 6 (12/12 IDENTICAL vs the frozen OLD lanes stays the definition of done; `legs_identical`
is the first component of BOTH tuples). Not a licence for rows: a row books nothing until the console image says so at that
pair, and the first row under it (`P8-SECOND-RING-OLD-PER-READ-MIRROR`, filed this stage) books large only where NEW == console.

**Rationale.** "This is what the hardware does": where the console confirms NEW and refutes OLD, the word is OLD's defect by
measurement, and a decision rule that counts it against NEW optimises toward the defect. Reporting the raw count keeps the gate's
honesty (nothing disappears; the user sees both numbers and the booked column); deciding on the unbooked count makes the loop
converge on the console.

**Toggle.** The `proof=console:` token on a row's directive (`verify/attribution/classes.tsv`); without any, unbooked == raw and the
rule is today's. **Measured outcome (P8.30-T, `int_work/p830t/rescore/`):** the first proved row `P8-SECOND-RING-OLD-PER-READ-MIRROR`
on coldboot b120, the directive the RING'S words `0x9493ec-0x968540` (cb `0xbab588` `[0x948540, 0x968540)` from `+0xeac`; the 41
heap words `0x968540-0x9685e4` past the ring's end that the earlier region rule reached are their own OPEN rule `SECOND-RING-PAST-END`,
2 differing words, no proof — review loop 1 trimmed the directive; the one booked word there, `0x9685dc`, left both sides alike) — key
OFF (S11's binary, b120 regenerated, total 432,515 = S11's): `SECOND-RING ROW 17886`, `DT-7: booked_console=4899 (… proof rows 17886,
console == OLD 3673, console == neither 9314, unreadable 0)` — the console confirms NEW on only 4,899 of the 17,886 ring words (NEW's
own gap, BUG-086, holds the rest); key ON (the ring-cache binary, total 434,841 = the gate's): `SECOND-RING ROW 19470`,
`booked_console=19470 (… console == OLD 0, console == neither 0)` — every differing ring word is the console's. So the booking follows
the console, not the row: a console-exact NEW books the class, a NEW that still differs from the console does not. S11 re-scored:
`tuple=(0, 20258815, -1)`, `tuple_unbooked=(0, 20253916, -1)` (booked 4,899); the ring-cache gate re-scored under the per-run pairing:
`tuple=(0, 20084447, -1)`, `tuple_unbooked=(0, 20064977, -1)` (booked 19,470); `score.py --compare` → `compare: A=(0, 20258815, -1)
B=(0, 20084447, -1) -> BETTER [REPORTED]` / `compare (DT-7, unbooked): A=(0, 20253916, -1) B=(0, 20064977, -1) -> BETTER [DECISION …;
booked A=4899 B=19470]` / `decision: BETTER` (`int_work/p830t/logs/compare_loop1.log`; the per-word audit `requote.py`: booked min/max
`0x9496cc/0x968530` OFF, `0x949408/0x96853c` ON, none outside the ring). **KEPT.**

**(1) The asset region's allocator (static, from the recomp + both images).** The region `0x01000000–0x01d00000` of INT-P825T-ATTRACT-HEAP-RELOCATION lies INSIDE the record pool `@pool 0x31b138` (base `0xf59ac0`, size `0x10a3d40` = 16.6 MiB to `0x1ffd800`) — the title's **compacting handle heap** (OLD `host_app/hle_meta_arena.h`: "the game's compacting handle heap … 0x20-byte block descriptors, the 0x18-byte compaction-job object, the 0xc-byte deferred-op nodes"; descriptors in the META arena `0x02000000`, `+0 handle idx, +4 buf, +8 size, +0x10 free link, +0x14 prev, +0x18 next, +0x1c flags`; header `+0 base, +4 size, +0xc..+0x48` the 16 free-list heads, `+0x4c` free count, `+0x50` the address-ordered chain head; live-record counter `0x323278`). The loader streams SUB-chunks of its package files into records (`int_work/p826b/rec_probe.py`: record `0xff4d00+0x880` = `Startup\Default.rm2 +0x108e66`, `0x14a99c0+0x40c0` = `Levels\Earth\Hub\beach.rm2 +0x17234b`, `0x1aa1540+0x940` = `Levels\Earth\Hub\huba.sm2 +0x158f2c`; the `0x2080/0x6080/0xa080` records are the streaming windows), so a record's identity is (package, offset) — `asset_names.py` (new tool: the archive index `DATA.BH` = 612 extents of `DATA.BD`, checked against the archive size, `names.tsv`) names only whole-file records (0 of 1830 here; the table serves the other legs' whole-file loads). **The compactor is the title's own:** `FUN_00205588` (one step = pick a job `FUN_00202560`, `memmove` the record `libc memmove 0x2c6d8c`, relink `FUN_00202908`) is called from the per-frame maintenance loop `FUN_0017d6b8` at `0x17d8bc` behind the headroom query `FUN_00191bd0(obj, f12 = 0.68 ms)` at `0x17d898` (`recomp/recompiled_shard_008.cpp:18617-18665`: `if (v0 != 0) { FUN_001816c8(); FUN_00205588(pool, 0); if (v0) s0 = 1 }`), next to the deferred-op processor `FUN_00203358` at `0x17d8d8` and the lap-end query `0x17d87c` (1.0 ms) — the loop runs compaction steps while the frame has headroom.

**(2) The five pairs (`relocate_compare.py --layout --names`, `int_work/p826b/reloc/b<o>_b<n>.{txt,json,blocks.tsv}`; NEW probes regenerated from a COPY of `build-release/app/host/nativeee` md5 `2503b75c…` of HEAD 9c4a1c2 by five one-image legs, `int_work/p826b/probe_runs.out`).** The pool walk honours the allocator's invariants on all ten images (free flags == free lists, free count, used == `0x323278`): **1830 used records on BOTH sides of every pair, the same used bytes `0xd5f8c0` and the same free bytes `0x344480`.**

Reading: (a) **relocation-equal 79–81 %** of every pair (3.13–3.21 M words) — the same bytes at other addresses, both sides explained; the `reloc-rehandled` column is NOT "another handle": on this leg `rehandled` = matched in the CONTENT pass, and **1029 of the 1108 content-pass records carry the SAME handle idx** on both sides (pass 1's exact equality failed on in-place differing words of records that only MOVED; 79 carry another handle) — corrected by the P8.26-B review (MAJOR 2) and split by P8.27-B (`rehandled-same-idx` / `-other-idx`, LEDGER P8.27-B (2)); (b) **the allocation ORDER is the same:** 1722 of the 1801 matched records carry the SAME handle idx on both sides (40 differ by −1, the rest are the recycled `0x2080` window handles 43–50 whose content coincides), the longest common handle order is 1764 of 1801 — the loads completed in the same sequence, so **the prompt's "load-completion order (DP-17)" hypothesis is REFUTED as the cause**; (c) yet 1751 of 1801 are at other ADDRESSES and only 805 (44.7 %) keep their relative address order; (d) **the compactor's progress differs:** NEW's pool is fully compacted at every probe (ONE free chunk `0x344480` at the top, used span `0xf59ac0–0x1cb9380`), OLD's carries **156 (155 free + 1 pending) → 90 → 14 holes** at b1500 / b2700 / b3780 (a `0x32040`-byte hole sliding upward `0x1330900 → 0x17fd140 → 0x1bb08c0` as records slide down) and is compacted only at b4200 / b5400 (1 hole, the SAME span as NEW's); (e) the first layout divergence is STILL used record #45 = OLD's SESSION-14 record (idx 53 `0x6080` at `0xfdcb00`, the four `0x6080` records idx 53–56 are live at the title; NEW fills that space with later small records idx 130/137/205/217/116/117/120 — first-fit into a hole) — the §7.1 row `P4-CKPT120-OLD-SESSION14-POPULATE-ALLOCS`; (f) `free-residue` 3.2 % → 1.6 % (DT-3 NONSTATE), `genuine` 3.1 → 4.8 % (record content differing in place — the title's state; grows with the leg), `other` 11.6–11.8 % = outside the arenas (the page pool: `SLAB-ALLOC-DIFFERS` 393–404 k words per pair, the largest OPEN class after the relocation). **Cause (named, OLD-side, two parts):** the SESSION-14 records (rowed) plus **OLD's host budget predicate starving the compactor** — OLD's `FUN_00191bd0` is `now = anchor + s_base_eff + reads_this_frame × step` (`host_app/main.cpp:3586-3640`, `s_base` 8800 of the 9600-tick frame), the class the row `P4-CKPT60-LOADWAIT-ISSUE-SLICE` already names "OLD-wrong (synthetic S96/S112 base, not a clock)" — so OLD runs a compaction step every ≈ 15 boundaries while the console's `FUN_00191bd0` reads T0 and grants the idle title frame's headroom (NEW: the recompiled predicate over the slice-frozen T0, DP-8 (1) option (i)). Since both sides are fully compacted at b4200 with the same records and the same order of allocation but 996 records out of address order, the permutation was fixed during the LOAD (compaction steps interleaved with the loader's allocs/frees at OLD's starved rate vs NEW's), and OLD's late compaction slides records without reordering them (the 805 / 996 split is identical on all five pairs). **No NEW placement gap found:** NEW's layout is its own allocator's compacted layout, every walk invariant holds on NEW's images. **NEW's rate is a model too (review MINOR 4):** the maintenance loop re-queries `FUN_00191bd0` after every compaction step (`0x17d8c8 → 0x17d898`); over NEW's slice-frozen T0 (DP-8 (1)(i)) the headroom answer is constant inside a frame, so NEW runs the compactor to exhaustion in the first frame that has jobs — "fully compacted at every probe" is NEW's within-frame loop behaviour, not a measured console rate (a console grants ≈ 0.68 ms of real T0 per frame and would compact over several frames). The pool LAYOUT is therefore a timing-dependent invariant on every tree, console included; what the row books is the same guest content at the allocator's other places, never "NEW = console".

**(3) The booking (honesty).** The directives `@reloc-class reloc-content/-rehandled → P4-CKPT120-OLD-SESSION14-POPULATE-ALLOCS ROW` were proved on coldboot b120's bump layout (no compaction step had run); on the attract pairs the relocation has the second cause above, so booking 3.1–3.2 M words per pair to that row would repeat P8.11-B's fiat (review MAJOR 3). `attribute_probe.py`'s `@reloc-class` gained an optional `legs` token (selftest: a leg-scoped directive books on its legs and leaves the class OPEN elsewhere); the two SESSION-14 directives are now scoped `coldboot`; the attract leg's relocation reads **OPEN** (`OPEN-RELOC-reloc-content` / `-rehandled`) until a row is filed. **Proposal (not decided): §7.1 row `P8-ATTRACT-OLD-COMPACTOR-STARVED`** (mem, coldboot_attract every probe: OLD's budget predicate starves the title's heap compactor (hole counts above) and its SESSION-14 records sit in the pool — the relocation-equal words are the same guest content at the allocator's other places), toggle = the two commented `@reloc-class … P8-ATTRACT-OLD-COMPACTOR-STARVED ROW coldboot_attract` lines in `classes.tsv`. The "+1" is the load duration's remainder modulo the window, not a boundary either tree adds at the attract trigger (the inter-edge distance is 2983 on both trees). So the whole content-pass class is explained by two NAMED causes (the compactor's schedule; the SESSION-14 handle shift / window-handle recycling) — the word-level class is honest as P8.26-B stated; only its documentation was wrong (corrected in README and LEDGER P8.26-B (2)).

**(3) The row proof — `P8-ATTRACT-OLD-COMPACTOR-STARVED` (proposal; the orchestrator files).**

*(a) The title's own code (the recomp; `recompiled_shard_008.cpp` Label_0044–0048, `recompiled_shard_017.cpp:27144–27360`).* The per-frame maintenance loop `FUN_0017d6b8` runs, at `0x17d898`, `f12 = 0x3a31c93b` (= **0.6782 ms**) → `FUN_00191bd0(obj)`; if v0 == 0 → `0x17d8d0` (deferred ops `FUN_00203358`, exit); else `0x17d8bc` `FUN_00205588(pool, 0)` = ONE compaction step (pick a job `FUN_00202560`, `memmove` the record `0x2c6d8c`, relink `FUN_00202908`; v0 = 1 if a record moved) and, at `0x17d8c8`, `if (v0 != 0) { s0 = 1; goto 0x17d898 }` — **the predicate is re-queried after EVERY step**; the loop ends when the predicate says no headroom or no job is left. `FUN_00191bd0` (the title's; `headroom_query_glue.h`): `ticks = (int)(f12 × obj->rate[+0x70])`, `quantum = rate / obj->fps[+0x68]` = 576000 / 60 = **9600 T0 counts**, `elapsed = (T0_now − obj->anchor[+0x80]) mod quantum`, `v0 = ticks < quantum − elapsed`; thresholds: `0x17d898` → **390 counts**, the lap-end query `0x17d868` (`0x3a83126f` = 1.000 ms) → 576 counts.

*(b) OLD's host predicate (read-only; `OLD/host_app/main.cpp:3586–3640`, the `PS2_BUDGET_EMU` default mode 1; `budget_math.h:99–108`):*
```
static uint32_t s_base_eff = 8800;                 // main.cpp:3586
static uint64_t s_last_anchor = ~0ull;
static uint32_t s_reads_this_frame = 0;
if (anchor != s_last_anchor) { s_last_anchor = anchor; s_reads_this_frame = 0; ...
    s_base_eff = s_phase.OnFrame(s_draws_in_frame, s_lowdraw_max, s_base_low, s_base, s_phase_on); ... }
++s_reads_this_frame;                              // main.cpp:3637
now = anchor + s_base_eff + (uint64_t)s_reads_this_frame * s_step;   // main.cpp:3638-3639 ; s_step = 96 (PS2_BUDGET_STEP), s_base = 8800 (PS2_BUDGET_BASE)
const bool headroom = budgetmath::HeadroomEmu(f12, rate, fps, now, anchor, phase);   // thresh < period - FoldClosed(now - anchor, period, phase)
```
OLD never reads T0 for this predicate: `now − anchor = 8800 + 96 × reads` — a constant "phantom render cost" (the comment at `main.cpp:3517-3528`: "the frame's deterministic EMULATED RENDER-WORK cost in counts") plus a per-QUERY decrement shared by every query site of the frame (the loader pump's, the lap's six, the compactor's). Arithmetic: remaining = 9600 − 8800 − 96 r = **800 − 96 r**; the compactor's 390-count threshold is met only while r ≤ 4, the lap-end 576 only while r ≤ 2 → **at most 4 grants per frame for ALL sites together**, and the compaction query comes late in the lap (after the lap's own queries), so it is granted rarely — and each grant buys exactly one step before the re-query at `0x17d8c8` costs another read. (The `PhaseClassifier` sets `base_low = 0` on frames with < 48 draws — the title and cutscene frames are full frames, so base 8800 applies; the `P4-CKPT60-LOADWAIT-ISSUE-SLICE` row names this exact predicate "OLD-wrong (synthetic S96/S112 base, not a clock)" = the DP-3 class.)

*(c) Hole counts and compaction rate per side (`pool_census.py`, `pool_census.out`; `census_leg.py` → `census/pool_census.out`).* OLD probes: b1500 **156 holes (155 free + 1 pending)**, b2700 **90**, b3780 **14**, b4200 **1**, b5400 **1**; records moved between consecutive probes (same idx, same size, other address — a lower bound on the steps, one record per step): b1500→b2700 **571 over 1200 boundaries (≥ 0.476 steps/boundary)**, b2700→b3780 **526 / 1080 (≥ 0.487)**, b3780→b4200 **50 / 420 (≥ 0.119; the pool is fully compacted somewhere in that span)**, b4200→b5400 **0** — OLD compacts the title's pool at ≈ 0.48 record moves per boundary, i.e. ≈ one step every 2 frames, for ≈ 3,000 boundaries after the load (OLD's loader allocates/frees at its wall-pumped rate (≈ 23 reads/frame) so the pool enters the title with ≥ 156 holes). NEW (one cold `--frames 1201 --state-every 1` leg from the stage's binary copy, 41 images kept and censused, lanes ≡ S10 rep 1 on all 1201 frames keys / 21 mem lines, image b1140's hash `0x77a33a637d7140fc` = S10's mem line 1140): the compactor runs **during the Beach load** — holes 42 (b600) → 36 → 31 → **1 (b675; 168 records moved in 25 boundaries ≥ 6.7 steps/boundary)** → 4 → 13 → 20 → 12 → 11 → 7 → 11 (b850) → 24 → 35 → 40 (b925) → 15 → 16 → 5 → 1 (b1025) → … → 3 (b1110) → 6 → 6 → 4 → 4 (b1130) → **1 (b1135)**, and from b1135 to b1200 (and on every later probe b1783…b5683) **1 hole, 0 records moved** — NEW's pool is fully compacted two boundaries after the scene edge (b1133) and never moves a record again inside the leg (the 2 moves P8.26-B's b1133→b1783 delta shows are b1133→b1135). Why NEW compacts to exhaustion: the recompiled predicate over the slice-frozen T0 (DP-8 (1)(i)) returns the same answer at every re-query inside a frame, so the `0x17d8c8 → 0x17d898` loop runs until `FUN_00205588` finds no job — NEW's rate is a MODEL of the predicate too (the console advances T0 in real time and grants ≈ 0.68 ms of steps per frame, several steps, over several frames): the pool LAYOUT is a timing-dependent invariant on every tree, console included.

*(d) The row.* **Class:** mem, leg `coldboot_attract`, every probe (b1500/b2700/b3780/b4200/b5400). **OLD-side cause — NAMED:** OLD's `FUN_00191bd0` hook answers the title's headroom query from a synthetic budget (`s_base_eff = 8800`, per-query 96-count decrement) instead of T0 — the predicate `P4-CKPT60-LOADWAIT-ISSUE-SLICE` already rows as "synthetic base, not a clock" (the DP-3 class) — starving the title's compactor (≤ 4 grants per frame shared with the loader pump; measured ≈ 0.48 record moves per boundary for ≈ 3,000 boundaries) while NEW's recompiled predicate compacts the pool to exhaustion during the load; plus the four SESSION-14 records live in the pool (rowed). **What the row books:** the both-sided relocation-explained words of matched pool records — `reloc-content` (1.16–1.19 M per pair) + `reloc-rehandled` (1.95–2.02 M, of which 93 % are records that kept their handle and only moved; the other-idx 7 % = the SESSION-14 handle shift (−1 × 40) and the recycled window handles — both named above) = 3.13–3.21 M words per pair (79–81 %), 15.8 M over the five pairs. NOT booked (stay OPEN, named): `genuine` (in-place state 121–191 k), `unpaired`/`unmatched`/one-sided records, the page-pool slab classes (DP-19 / SESSION-14 unobserved OLD allocations), `RECORD-POOL-HANDLE-TABLE`/`-HEADER`/`-MAP-SHIFT` (the layout's META consequences — they would follow the row if the orchestrator extends it). **Toggle (the directive text, inactive in `classes.tsv`):**
```
@reloc-class reloc-content POOL-RELOC-CONTENT P8-ATTRACT-OLD-COMPACTOR-STARVED ROW coldboot_attract
@reloc-class reloc-rehandled POOL-RELOC-REHANDLED P8-ATTRACT-OLD-COMPACTOR-STARVED ROW coldboot_attract
```
(booking the WHOLE `reloc-rehandled` class; if the orchestrator prefers to book only the same-idx records, `reloc-rehandled-other-idx` must first become a CLASS of its own in `relocate_compare.CLASSES` — a one-line change proposed, not made: the review asked to document, not re-engineer). **Verdict on filability:** the OLD-side cause is named and quoted (b), its mechanism measured on both trees (c), the words are guest content at the allocator's other places (never a mask), the toggle exists → **filable** under EQUIVALENCE_PLAN §7.1, with the (c) caveat in the row text that NEW's within-frame exhaustion is a model and the layout is timing-dependent on the console too.

### LEDGER — P8.28-B: the `P8-ATTRACT-OLD-COMPACTOR-STARVED` row FILED with its proof corrected by measurement (the P8.27-B review's MINOR 1: WHICH base governed OLD's load — base 0, and the starvation there is the lap sites' per-query cost, not the base), the five attract pairs re-run with the directives active, frames Class B at the title after BUG-084, the `VifArmsVsOld` under-load failure named and fixed test-side (2026-10-05; `int_work/p828b/`; tools `tools/vif_replay/vif_replay.cpp` (the handshake + the seeded fault), `verify/attribution/classes.tsv` (directives active), `docs/design/EQUIVALENCE_PLAN.md` §7.1 (the row); helpers `old_budget_diag_leg.py`, `budget_diag_stats.py`, `title_classb.py`, `classb_draw_join.py`, `vifarms_load.sh`, `probe_runs.py`, `ledger_runs.sh`)

**(1) MINOR 1 resolved by OLD's code AND an OLD measurement (OLD read-only, run bounded as an oracle through `run_leg.py --tree legacy` with the log-only `PS2_BUDGET_DIAG=2000000` armed — I8.3-G's recipe; `int_work/p828b/olddiag/leg.log`, 35,321 `[BUDGET-EMU]` lines over vf 60–1999; a second run with `--state-every 60` for the pool census, `oldimg/`).** The code: `OLD/host_app/main.cpp:3566-3570` `s_phase_on = !v || !*v || atoi(v) != 0` (ON when `PS2_BUDGET_PHASE` is unset — the frozen baseline's `run.meta env_ps2` does not set it), `:3571-3582` `s_lowdraw_max` 48 / `s_base_low` 0, `:3586` `s_base_eff = 8800`, `:3589-3598` at every anchor change `s_draws_in_frame = g_draws_total − s_draws_at_anchor; s_base_eff = s_phase.OnFrame(s_draws_in_frame, 48, 0, 8800, s_phase_on)`, `budget_math.h:139-159` `if (draws_in_frame < lowdraw_max) ++low_streak else low_streak = 0; low = enabled && low_streak >= kConfirmFrames (2); return low ? base_low : base_hi`; `g_draws_total` counts DrawBatch + SetFrontBuffer + UploadVRAMTexture pushes (`render.h:3195-3204`). The measurement (`budget_diag_stats.py`; the run is a Shape-A run — edge #7 at vf 1371, mem differs from b600 — but the base is a function of the title's draws per frame and OLD's code, not of the host timing): **the Beach load (boot SM case 6, vf 970–1371 = b440–b841)**: `dpf` median 25, `ph=1` on 397/399 frames (base **0**, first-query `rem=9504`), in the allocation span vf 1071–1371 (b540–b840) `dpf=25 ph=1` on **301/301** frames with **97 queries per frame**; **the cutscene (case 7, vf 1372–1999)**: `dpf` median 67 (min 25, max 354), `ph=0` on **628/629** frames (base **8800**, first-query `rem=704`), 5 queries per frame. So the P8.27-B sentence "base 8800 → ≤ 4 grants per frame for ALL sites" holds for the cutscene/title and NOT for the load. **The grants under the base that applied:** *(load, base 0)* one frame's dump (vf 1200): `17d724 1.6953ms rem=9504 hr=1`, `17d724 9408 hr=1`, `17d7a4 1.3562 9312 hr=1`, `17d814 1.0172 9216 hr=1`, then the four lap sites `17d87c / 17d75c / 17d7d8 / 17d848` (`f12=0.0010000` = 576 counts) round-robin `9120 … 672` all `hr=1` (92 queries), then `576 hr=0`, `480 hr=0`, `384 hr=0` (each DENIED query still costs 96), and the compactor's `17d8ac f12=0.0006781 rem=288 hr=0` — over the span: compactor queries 323, `rem=288` 269× (denied), 384 28× (denied), 480 16× (granted) → **26 grants in 301 frames (8 %)**; the lap sites 20,967 queries 96 % granted. *(cutscene/title, base 8800)* `704/608/512` deny the three pump sites, the compactor's first query at `rem=416 ≥ 390` granted, its re-query at 320 denied → **exactly one step per frame: 628 grants / 628 frames** (1,231 queries). *(before the load, the FMV loops, base 0, 3–4 queries/frame)*: the compactor is granted at will (739/739 in vf 970–1070 incl. a 92-step burst at vf 1007). **The pool census on the run's images (`pool_census.py`, `oldimg/pool_census.out`, `pool_census_load.out`; b480 and b720 fail the free-list walk mid-allocation):** FMV loops b120 → b300: holes 41 → 23 → **1** (30 / 215 / 28 moves: compaction keeps up); load b540 → b600 → b660 → b780 → b840: used 360 → 1078 → 1265 → 1802 → 1830, **holes 3 → 118 → 132 → 180 → 180, moved 1 / 0 / 5 / 4** (the compactor does not run); after the edge b840 → b900 → b960 → b1020: holes 180 → 178 → 177 → 177, **moved 28 / 28 / 26 per 60 boundaries (≈ 0.45/boundary)** = the one-step-per-frame rate when a job exists; the frozen probes continue it (156 at b1500 → 90 → 14 → 1; 571 / 526 / 50 moves, P8.27-B). NEW compacts during its load (holes ≤ 42, 1 hole from b1135, P8.27-B (3)(c)). **The proof sentence, corrected:** OLD's predicate never reads T0 (`now = anchor + s_base_eff + reads × 96`); it starves the compactor in BOTH phases by two arithmetics — during the load the four 1.0-ms lap sites spend the base-0 field query by query (92 granted + 3 denied × 96 counts) so the compactor asks at 288/384 < 390; in the cutscene/title the 8800 base leaves 800 and the compactor's second query falls below 390 → one step per frame. Both are the DP-3 class (`P4-CKPT60-LOADWAIT-ISSUE-SLICE`: "synthetic base, not a clock"). **Row FILED** in `docs/design/EQUIVALENCE_PLAN.md` §7.1 after `P7-FMV-BVOL-OLD-UNSET` (`diff_legs.known_row_ids()` 251 → **252**, `P8-ATTRACT-OLD-COMPACTOR-STARVED` resolves); the two `@reloc-class … ROW coldboot_attract` directives ACTIVATED in `verify/attribution/classes.tsv` (the comment above them corrected to the measured sentence); `attribute_probe.py --selftest` PASS, `relocate_compare.py --selftest` PASS.

OPEN, no new row (no OLD-side cause).

**(3) `VifArmsVsOld` under load — NAMED: a TEST defect (a scheduling lottery in the tool's liveness assertion), not an engine race; FIXED test-side.** The failing line comes from `tools/vif_replay/vif_replay.cpp` `mtvu_arm::Run` (`:1091-1097` before the fix): after 20 iterations, `total_eager_early == 0` → `MTVU EQUIVALENCE FAIL: eager-drain starvation …`. `eager_early` counts the packets found in `rig.drained` BEFORE pass 3's final `rig.arbiter.drain()`, i.e. drained by `rig.sync.TryEagerDrain()` — called once after each chain's submit and once per spin of the `while (poll_stat_once()) { TryEagerDrain(); yield(); }` loop, where `poll_stat_once` is `MtvuSync::TryVif1StatBusyView` = "the worker is not idle" (`mtvu_sync.cpp:53-58`) and `TryEagerDrain` drains only when `arbiter_.hasSealedReady()` (`:61-65`). There is NO wall clock in that path (the only clock, the 10-s convergence guard, never fired) and no engine wait: the count is non-zero only if this thread observes a SEALED marker while the worker is still busy or before its next poll — which depends entirely on how the host interleaves the two threads. Alone, the worker (its spin tier armed by recency, `mtvu_worker.cpp:230-246`) seals chain k before the EE's `TryEagerDrain` → 72–100 of 120 packets early (15–19 of 20 iterations; `int_work/p828b/vifarms/alone_r*.log`). Under load the worker thread is descheduled at submit, the EE submits all three chains and yields, the worker then runs its batched queue (`local.swap(queue_)`, `:258-263`) to completion while the EE is off the CPU, the EE's next poll sees idle, `Sync()` takes the idle fast path (`mtvu_sync.cpp:24-29`, no drain) → 0 early packets. Reproduced from the stage's COPY of the tool (`vifarms_load.sh`, 16 × `yes > /dev/null`, killed after): 12 pkts (3/20 iters), 10 (2/20), **FAIL 0 pkts** as the load average rose 10 → 32 (`vifarms/load16_r*.log`); the equivalence checks (packet census + order, VU1 memory, boundaries position-exact) PASSED in every rep — only the liveness heuristic failed. The engine's seal wake is sound by reading: `SealAndRetire` bumps `seal_count_` seq_cst and, when `sync_waiters_ > 0`, takes the lock and `notify_all`s `cv_idle_` (`mtvu_worker.cpp:189-200`); `WaitIdleOrSeal` re-checks `seal_count_ != seen` under the same mutex (`:313-319`) → no lost wake; and the failing run never entered that path. **Fix (test-side, `tools/vif_replay/vif_replay.cpp`, pass 3):** a deterministic handshake — after each chain's submit the EE parks on the worker's progress signal (`EnterSyncWait(); while (SealCount() == seen && !WorkerIdle()) WaitIdleOrSeal(MtvuSync::kPollNs, seen); LeaveSyncWait();` — the same `cv_idle_` seal wake `Sync`'s eager loop uses; the loop ends on the worker's progress, never on a clock) and only then `TryEagerDrain()`s, so the sealed prefix is there to drain whatever the scheduling; the assertion now means "the seal → ready → eager-drain path delivered" (its comment rewritten). A seeded fault `--mtvu-fault-no-eager` (pass 3 never eager-drains) proves the assertion still bites: `MTVU EQUIVALENCE FAIL: eager-drain starvation - 0 packets drained mid-wait across 20 iterations (seal wake broken?)`, exit 1. The tool rebuilt in a PRIVATE build dir (`int_work/p828b/build_vr`, Ninja Debug, target `vif_replay` + the four `tests/vif` binaries; `build/` untouched): ALONE 115–117 / 120 pkts early (20/20 iters) × 3 PASS; under 16 × `yes` (load 10 → 35) 119–120 / 120 × 3 PASS; under 24 × `yes` (load 35 → **73**) 112–120 / 120 × 3 PASS; `vif_arms_vs_old.py --arms all` against the OLD tool: `VIFARMS arms=6 same=6 diff=0` (the eager-drained count and the iteration ratio are blanked timing tokens on both sides; the OLD arm's lines are unchanged); `ctest --test-dir build_vr -R 'Verify|Vif'`: the 12 Vif tests (incl. `VifArmsVsOld 13.27 s`) and the 16 Verify selftests PASS (the 4 other matches are binaries not built in the private dir: 3 Not Run + `RootRegistersVuVifGifSections` without a `nativeee`). Nothing weakened: the equivalence pin is byte-for-byte the same check; the liveness check went from scheduling-dependent to deterministic.

What would unblock: (i) an OLD-side census of the page pool's F:96 allocations (the `SLAB-ALLOC-DIFFERS` 394 k — OLD's `[ALLOC-TRACE]` covers the record pool only), (ii) the `--watch-word` census leg on the first `GENUINE` records (the 121–191 k in-place words: a NEW diag leg plus the OLD writer), (iii) the one-sided / unmatched records (≈ 56 k: OLD's 4 × `0x6080` + NEW's populate records, named but not separable per word), (iv) the ≈ 1.9 k UNATTRIBUTED at `0x3b1000–0x3b3000`. ### LEDGER — P8.29-B: the CONSOLE arbitrates coldboot b120's OPEN classes (2026-10-05; `int_work/p829b/`; tool NEW `verify/console_compare.py` + ctest `VerifyConsoleCompareSelftest`; images `int_work/p828a/pcsx2_rec159_ee.bin` (the P8.28-A matched state) and `int_work/p829b/pcsx2_rec159_plus5.p2s` (5 frames on, loader idle); no engine edit, no row filed, nothing decided; CORRECTED per its review loop 1 — the tool's arena list made symmetric and its 4 KB fingerprint cap lifted, every number below re-run from the corrected tool (`compare_rec159.txt` / `compare_plus5.txt`, header line `fingerprint: the WHOLE object; pointer arenas (ONE list, every side): 0x3dae90-0xb84be0 0xb8c560-0xf58560 0xf59ac0-0x1ffd800 0x2000000-0x2200000`); CORRECTED AGAIN per its review loop 2 — the four `0x6080` records described BY CONTENT (OLD's four = copies of hardware's idx-99 record with a non-zero tail; hardware's idx 54/56/58 absent on OLD), the tool's META span derived from the images (the `0x200000` literal removed; `LintEnginePurity` PASS), the selftest at 20 checks)

The trees' b120 images are what the lanes recorded (NEW `0x6371becdb140620a`).

**Construction fact the compare must set aside first (INT-P829B-CONSOLE-DESCRIPTORS-ON-SLAB; rec159 numbers):** the console keeps the record pool's descriptors on the shared PAGE POOL — `records con: … desc in RAM 385 / META 0` vs the trees' `desc in RAM 0 / META 385` — as 32-byte chunks of slab class 7 (`console record descriptors live in {'cls 7/32B': 385}`, spread over 12 pages `6, 43, 44, 45, 48, 55, 62, 119, 131, 132, 141, 150`, interleaved with the title's 32-byte path-string nodes `Levels\Earth\Hub\Beach`, `SkyDomes\`, `Materials\`, `Textures\` …), plus the `0x18`-byte compaction job (class 5/24B: `con-only 1`) and the `0xc`-byte deferred-op nodes (class 2/12B: `con-only 4`) — exactly the three node kinds the META-ARENA workaround (HOOK row `0x181560`, `games/…/hle/workarounds/meta_arena.h`) routes OUT of the slab in both trees. Consequence: class 7 holds 12 pages on the console vs 9/9 (used chunks 1,455 vs 1,075/1,068; the console's NON-descriptor 32-byte objects number 1,455 − 385 = 1,070 ≈ the trees' 1,075/1,068), and every later page taken from the LIFO free-page stack is shifted by those three pages — **the page→class ASSIGNMENT of the console cannot equal either tree's while the workaround is active**; object CONTENT sets can.

**The decision (the user's, verbatim in substance; `int_work/SHARED.md` "PHASE 8 PRODUCT HALF").** (1) The memory-identity goal against OLD is RETIRED. OLD is NOT the reference — the PCSX2 console is; where NEW and OLD disagreed the console has mostly sided with NEW (the P8.29-B / P8.32-B arbitration tables: the P8.32-B status table at `:1775-1786`: 5 rows console≡NEW, 1 console≡OLD — the type-registry gap, since fixed as BUG-089 — 1 console≡both, 2 neither, 2 timing). The final OPEN-class ledger is written AS-IS (`## LEDGER — FINAL (P8.35-C)` below; `docs/phases/PHASE8_LEDGER_FINAL.md`) and Phase 8's equivalence half is CLOSED. (2) Standing gates, every builder stage running the ones its change can affect and the orchestrator the full set per commit: **(i)** the 13-leg battery as a REGRESSION gate only — `gate_run.py --pairing run --classes verify/attribution/classes.tsv`, 3 reps; the score tuple (raw AND unbooked) must NOT worsen vs the last gate of record and every leg's frames/audio FIRST-DIVERGENT key must stay at or above its current value; the reference table is `verify/baseline/REGRESSION_REF.tsv` (written by P8.35-C from S12; moves with the frozen legs in P8.36-D), `score.py --regression REF.tsv candidate/score.json` decides, `gate_run.py` prints `regression: PASS|FAIL vs REGRESSION_REF.tsv` in SUMMARY.md; the mem lane is REPORTED, never a decision criterion; **(ii)** the PCSX2 GS-dump replay tests (`Render_TitleFrameReplay*`, `RootTitleScreenEffects`, `RootAttractCutsceneFrames`, every later dump-backed test) are THE rendering CORRECTNESS gate — a visual defect against PCSX2 is a bug, fixed by a stage with a replay pin; **(iii)** `verify/console_compare.py` arbitrates new disputes (a saved PCSX2 EE image at a matched moment vs NEW's). A visual or audible defect against PCSX2 OUTRANKS any memory-word count. The full ctest suite ALONE, the lints (`LintOneClass`, `LintEnginePurity`, `CoreSingleGetenvSite`, the new size-budget and header-globals lints), 0 warnings, Release rebuilt + `RootReleaseLanesIdentical` stay mandatory per stage. **DT-13 — CUTOVER ACCEPTANCE (user, 2026-10-09 08:46).** "Frames/audio identical to REFERENCE" is NOT a blocking criterion for a cutover flip — a native path legitimately differs from the emulated one it replaces. Blocking: (1) Release build clean and ctest green (incl. the frozen anchors loading); (2) the acceptance leg and both smokes run to completion (no abort, no fault, no refusal storm); (3) the replaced model's census = 0 with owned work positive. Frames/audio deltas vs REFERENCE are RECORDED (first divergent key, magnitude via frame_diff / audio window delta) as information for the user's play test, not as a verdict. Flips 8 (graphics_movie) and 9 (music) re-run under DT-13; flips 1/2/3/6/7/10 stay reverted (real failures) pending the shared-cause attribution for the Graphics bridge. The user judges output when playing; PCSX2 re-enters only through user-reported bugs (DT-12).

**DT-14 — CLOCK CONTRACT (user, 2026-10-10 19:34; integrated 2026-10-10 23:3x, `main dc1a8a3a`; `int_work/m_series/CUTOVER.md` §DT-14, the stage record `int_work/m_series/cutover/allon_clock/SECTION.md`).** Game speed is constant real time; frame rate is whatever the renderer achieves. The s5 symptoms on `ff12dca8` (`play_sessions/s5/run.meta`: `pace_parks=2602 pace_late=2366`, `gs_queue frame_waits=4963 max_depth=2984`, `audio dropped=344896 underruns=1060` — visible speed-up/slow-down, pitch following) are the game thread waiting on the renderer and the audio ring dropping when the game ran ahead. **The contract (play mode only; the deterministic profile is unchanged):** (1) the game boundary tick fires at exactly 60 Hz WALL time, never faster — an early EE sleeps to the next 1/60 s edge (`TickThread` edges = anchor + k·1e9/60 ns, rational; `BoundarySequencer` wall-locked slot, every noted tick fired 1:1, no surplus tick, `EmuDomain::OnBoundary` no quantum top-up); (2) the renderer NEVER blocks the game thread — `frame_waits=0`, at most ONE pending frame in the GS queue, the OLDER pending frame is DROPPED when the next is submitted (`DrawIrThreadQueue::SetFrameSkip`, `frames_dropped=`), the bridge's per-group fence is the stream position (`hooks.delivery.drain` returns without `Sync()` under frame skip, `graphics_bridge_drain=stream_ordered=,waited=`), owned deliveries run on the consumer without the EE waiting (`RunOrderedAsync`, `graphics_owned_async=`), and the EE's local→local VRAM source is served EE-side (`ServeLocalToLocalSource`: EE image authoritative, GPU-target pages from the consumer's last completed frame snapshot, staleness in `gs_queue_l2l=`) — the only `Sync()` callers left are the F5 savestate capture, shutdown, the deterministic-profile drain and the uncounted leg-end `Quiesce()`; (3) a late EE reports the elapsed vsync count the way the PS2 did (a 33 ms boundary fires 2 fields → `DerivedClocks::T0Counts` +2×9600 → the title's GameTime delta doubles; TR `src/platform/ps2/time.cpp` Platform::Time::Ticks, `src/game/clock.cpp` GameTimeTick→GameTimeSetStamp) — wall speed with a larger step, never slow motion; (4) audio is driven by the WALL clock — the device pulls at 48 kHz, the mixer produces per real 60 Hz tick; a short pull repeats the previous quantum's tail (`AudioPullRing` pad mode, `padded=`, re-latched at the next push `relatches=`, a late catch-up burst trimmed back to the lead `trimmed=`) — never pitch-shift, never drop; (5) census series per 1 s wall window — `game_hz=` (boundaries, attributed to the window of the boundary's first field's wall EDGE, so `game_hz ≤ 60` by construction and 60 means every edge answered), `field_hz=`, `render_fps=`, plus `frames_dropped=`, `pace_late_fields=`, `pace_slept_ms=`, `pace_late_trace=ordinal:work_us:fields` (the first 64 two-field boundaries = the EE's OWN frame work), `mem_lane=` (off in play: the harness's 34 MB guest-RAM FNV every 60 boundaries on the game thread was a systematic 38 ms frame); the F1 panel shows `game N Hz | render M fps`. **Pins:** `RootClockContract` (the s3 fixture replay, 1500 frames: `frame_waits=0`, `ordered_waits=0`, `sync_wait_ms=0`, bridge `waited=0`, `mem_lane=off`, `domain tick == pacer ticks`, `absorbed=0`, audio `dropped=0 underruns=0`, `field_hz 58..62` in every in-loop window, **`game_hz 59.5..60.5` in every in-loop window** except a window whose fields outnumber its boundaries — an `EE_OWN_TIME` window, a frame the EE's own work did not finish in a period with the renderer provably uninvolved — bounded by `--max-own-time-windows` 3, printed), `PacerReleaseSequencer`, `DrawIrThreadQueueFrameSkip`, `DrawIrThreadQueueLocalToLocalSource`, `AudioPullRing`, `ClockCensus`, `RootPlayWallLock`, `RootGsQueuePipelinedIdentical` (the deterministic profile's identity). **Measured on the integrated Release (`a9664e6f…` / frozen `nativeee_release.dc1a8a3a`, idle machine, one game process; `allon_clock/s{3,5}_play/leg/run.meta`):** s3 padlog 3000-frame play replay `--harness-window`: `gs_queue syncs=0 frame_waits=0 ordered_waits=0 ordered_wait_ms=0 sync_wait_ms=0 frame_skip=1`, `graphics_bridge_drain waited=0`, `frames_dropped=3`, `audio_ring dropped=0 underruns=0` (`pushed=5142400 pulled=5138432 padded=6912 relatches=6 trimmed=8576`), `domain tick=6419 == tick_thread ticks=6419`, `resyncs=0`, `pace_late=31/3000 pace_late_fields=9`, game_hz over ALL 106 windows **0/60** (the zeros are the FMV `fmv_window=399-2972` and the level load), **in-loop (44 windows) 59/60**, histogram 60→42, 59→2, field_hz 60 in every in-loop window, render_fps 55–61; the two 59 windows are `pace_late_trace` boundaries `1248:29972:2` and `1885:43965:2` (30 / 44 ms of EE own work, both delivered as 2 fields). s5 padlog: same zero wait rows, `frames_dropped=2`, `dropped=0 underruns=0`, `tick 4182 == 4182`, `pace_late=10`, ALL 69 windows **0/60**, **in-loop (43) 58/60**, histogram 60→41, 59→1, 58→1, field_hz 60 everywhere; the two misses are `1248:30298:2` and `1352:56026:3`. (On `ff12dca8`: s5 `pace_late=2366 frame_waits=4963 dropped=344896 underruns=1060`.) Deterministic 1300-frame coldboot vs main's binary: `diff_legs: IDENTICAL` (frames 1300 keys, audio 217, mem 22). **DONE status (the parent's reading):** rules 1, 2, 4, 5 are met whole on both replays (every renderer-wait row 0; dropped=0; underruns=0; field_hz 60 in every in-loop window = no slow motion, no pitch change); the literal "game_hz 59.5–60.5 in EVERY 1 s window" is met in 42/44 (s3) and 41/43 (s5) in-loop windows — each miss is ONE boundary whose EE/host own-frame-time exceeded 16.7 ms (`sync_wait_ms=0`: not a renderer coupling; rule 3 delivered its fields), the systematic one at boundary **1248** (~30 ms on s3, s5 and the pin alike; `footprint_series` +49 MB between b1200 and b1260, no loader request — owner unknown) and one sporadic 44–56 ms frame per replay (loader burst / unattributed). These are perf-attribution items, recorded here as the carve-out `RootClockContract` tolerates (≤3 own-time windows per 1500-frame fixture replay), NOT a clock-contract defect — the user decides whether the documented carve-out satisfies the DONE or funds the 1248 attribution. The LIVE play run (the DONE's third leg) is the user's. The F5 routing fix rides in the same binary (below). **IN FORCE** for play mode; the deterministic profile keeps every wait (`frame_waits=1300 frame_skip=0`, `mem_lane=on`).

**DT-14 companion — F5 ROUTING (user, 2026-10-10 19:34 "Also"; Bernoulli `port-product` 4ae95ea2..5c2de09b; `input_saves/F5_ROUTING_REVIEW.md` APPROVED).** Attribution: macOS does not always activate the harness-launched process; SDL then never records a keyboard-focus window and stamps every key event `windowID=0`; `SessionGui::ProcessEvent` at 8bd76e91 discarded a press whose `windowID != window_id_` BEFORE `HandleFunctionKey`, silently — not a mapper swallow. Fix (`1b9245dc`): a key press is ours unless SDL names a window that is not ours (`windowID == window_id_ || windowID == 0 || SDL_GetWindowFromID(windowID) != nullptr`), `SessionWindow::Open` requests focus (`SDL_RaiseWindow` + `SDL_SetWindowInputFocus`), a hotkey the GUI did not consume prints `nativeee: F<n> discarded: SDL stamped window id U (session window W)` (never silent) and counts `hotkeys=key_events=,function_keys=,discarded=` in run.meta; `SessionRuntime::InjectFunctionKey(n)` pushes a REAL `SDL_KEYDOWN/KEYUP` pair into SDL's queue (`harness.inject_key=F5@N`, `--set` only, needs `--harness-window`). Pins: `SessionHotkeyRoute` (the deterministic red: the `windowID=0` case fails on the old router every run), `RootPlayHotkeyF5` (the real SDL route end to end: `savestate F5 save Completed`, `slot0.ps2st` written, `discarded=0`; red on the old router whenever macOS withholds focus — reproducible, not deterministic, stated in `303c75a5`). Integrator change: the `[sdl-trace]` attribution trace is armed by `--set dev.log_level=narrate` (`SessionRuntime::SetEventTrace`), not a `PS2R_SDL_EVENT_TRACE` getenv — `CoreSingleGetenvSite` allows one site (`dc1a8a3a`). A physical-key spot check by the user on the frozen binary is the remaining leg.

**Track 1d — `video.scale` defaults to 1 in play (2026-10-11, Zeno `port-graphics-r2` 0c8ecf55; record `int_work/m_series/graphics/track1d/TRACK1D.md`, in-tree PERFORMANCE.md §10).** The compiled play default follows the deterministic profile: scale 1 is the byte-identity contract AND the player's composition; 2..4 remain the option (`--set video.scale=2`; the F1 display settings are unchanged). Consequences: the deterministic fingerprint no longer carries `video.scale=1` (a cross-binary `diff_legs` against a pre-1d build declares `--allow-set-difference video.scale`, as `hle.startup` was declared across the 3a boundary); `RootShadowLevelEntry` judges the presented frame at the default (its boxes were always stated on the 512×448 logical frame and scaled to the image) and the suite keeps the scale-2 arm as `RootShadowLevelEntryScale2` (the s4 shape reproduces only there). The same stage's level-route profile and the pure-perf fixes it funded (the IMAGE upload store staged per column and walked per row segment, the frames-lane hash taken on the consumer, the owned DrawIr, the DrawIR queue signalling only a sleeper, the game thread at the interactive QoS, the decode reading its footprint, the mirror import walking its band) are recorded there with the deterministic identity on the coldboot and the 7600-frame level route; `ee_cpu_ms=` (the game thread's CPU per wall window) is the census row that reads the EE's cost on a loaded machine.
